# Battle Chronicles - commander phase events.
#
# New additive file. CK3 reads every file in common/combat_phase_events/ and
# merges the keys, so nothing here replaces vanilla, AGOT or Battlefield Duel+.
#
# Firing model, all verified in game:
#   - a phase event rolls every COMBAT_EVENT_DAYS = 5 while a battle runs
#   - `type = commander` roots on the commander, so `is_ai = no` means the PLAYER
#     is personally leading that army - it is not a "player is watching" test
#   - the chance is a weight against every OTHER VALID event, per the engine's own
#     `_combat_phase_events.info`. The pool is vanilla `commander_none` at
#     base = 1000 plus AGOT's casualty events
#   - bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger, not the weights, is what stops these firing
#     back to back; see bc_scripted_effects.txt
#
# STATE FIRST, PRESENTATION SECOND. Every momentum change used to live inside the
# `send_interface_toast` block. That is not safe: a toast is documented as
# "sends a message to the player playing the character in the scope and then
# executes any effects inside", and most commanders in the world are AI, with no
# player playing them. Left as it was, the entire momentum system risked applying
# to the player's battles and to nobody else's - and bc_lines_bogged_down proved
# the asymmetry, because it penalised the enemy commander from outside a toast
# while penalising its own commander from inside one. Vanilla keeps state changes
# in the effect block and messages purely presentational; so do we now.
#
# WEIGHTS, retuned after the first live session (59 commander fires):
#   banner_falls 10, rally 5, horse_killed 15, flank 7, van 16, ground 5, bogged 1
# horse_killed has the weakest situational gate of the seven and was firing like
# a default, so its base drops to 40. bogged_down's terrain gate is narrow enough
# that it needs a higher base to appear at all. bc_frequency exists so the rest
# stays retunable without a script edit.

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The banner falls. The signature setback: the army loses its focal point.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_banner_falls = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Plan 4: the read-back. A grudge match produces more moments than a
		# battle against a stranger. Same factor on every commander event - a
		# per-event tuning pass needs its own runtime measurement.
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_banner_falls"

		if = {
			limit = { bc_can_interrupt_player_trigger = yes }
			bc_start_popup_cooldown_effect = yes
			trigger_event = bc_chronicles.0001
		}
		else = {
			bc_faltering_major_effect = yes
			add_stress = 10
			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = major KIND = wild SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_banner_falls_toast
				desc = bc_banner_falls_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_commander
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Rally the line. The only way back out of a setback, so it gates on having one.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_rally_the_line = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A capable commander is likelier to find the moment.
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			martial >= 12
		}
		# Plan 4: the read-back. A grudge match produces more moments than a
		# battle against a stranger. Same factor on every commander event - a
		# per-event tuning pass needs its own runtime measurement.
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_rally_the_line"

		if = {
			limit = { bc_can_interrupt_player_trigger = yes }
			bc_start_popup_cooldown_effect = yes
			trigger_event = bc_chronicles.0002
		}
		else = {
			bc_rally_effect = yes
			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_rally_the_line_toast
				desc = bc_rally_the_line_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_commander
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A horse killed under him. Non-lethal by design decision - no death, no maiming
# anywhere in this mod. The commander is shaken, and is admired for standing up.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_horse_killed_under_him = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }
		prowess >= 8
	}

	# Lowest base of the seven: this is the only one with no situational gate at
	# all, so at base = 60 it behaved like a second `none` event.
	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Fighting commanders are the ones who get unhorsed.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 14
		}
		# Plan 4: the read-back. A grudge match produces more moments than a
		# battle against a stranger. Same factor on every commander event - a
		# per-event tuning pass needs its own runtime measurement.
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_horse_killed_under_him"

		bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		add_prestige = 50
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = bc_horse_killed_toast
			desc = bc_horse_killed_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A flank collapses. Needs a real numbers disadvantage, not just "behind".
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_flank_collapses = {
	type = commander

	# The tier gate matters here as much as anywhere: bc_faltering_minor and
	# bc_faltering_major are different modifier keys, so without it this event
	# could land a -6 on top of a live -3 and put the commander at -9, off the
	# end of the ladder the whole mod is tuned against.
	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }
		bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Plan 4: the read-back. A grudge match produces more moments than a
		# battle against a stranger. Same factor on every commander event - a
		# per-event tuning pass needs its own runtime measurement.
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_flank_collapses"

		# THE FOURTH POP-UP BRANCH, 2026-08-17, and the first template to grow
		# one since plan 1. Shape copied verbatim from bc_banner_falls above:
		# no rung is applied before the raise, because the option is the
		# outcome, and the cooldown is started at the call site rather than
		# inside the event.
		#
		# THE `else` ARM IS THE UNCHANGED ORIGINAL. Every AI commander and
		# every player inside the 60-day pop-up cooldown still gets exactly the
		# decisive setback and the toast this template has always given, which
		# is what keeps the change additive rather than a retune.
		if = {
			limit = { bc_can_interrupt_player_trigger = yes }
			bc_start_popup_cooldown_effect = yes
			trigger_event = bc_chronicles.0004
		}
		else = {
			bc_faltering_major_effect = yes
			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = decisive KIND = wild SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_flank_collapses_toast
				desc = bc_flank_collapses_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_commander
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

		NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes }
		martial >= 10

	}

	chance = {
		base = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			martial >= 16
		}
		# Plan 4: the read-back. A grudge match produces more moments than a
		# battle against a stranger. Same factor on every commander event - a
		# per-event tuning pass needs its own runtime measurement.
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_van_breaks_through"

		if = {
			limit = { bc_can_interrupt_player_trigger = yes }
			bc_start_popup_cooldown_effect = yes
			trigger_event = bc_chronicles.0003
		}
		else = {
			bc_momentum_major_effect = yes
			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = major KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_van_breaks_through_toast
				desc = bc_van_breaks_through_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_commander
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The lines bog down. The only symmetrical event: both commanders suffer, which
# is what makes bad ground read as bad ground rather than as a penalty aimed at
# the player.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_lines_bogged_down = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }
		bc_terrain_bogs_us_down_trigger = yes
	}

	# Fired once in a whole session. The terrain gate is narrow enough on its own
	# that it does not also need a low weight.
	chance = {
		base = 100
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Plan 4: the read-back. A grudge match produces more moments than a
		# battle against a stranger. Same factor on every commander event - a
		# per-event tuning pass needs its own runtime measurement.
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_lines_bogged_down"

		bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = bc_lines_bogged_down_toast
			desc = bc_lines_bogged_down_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE SECOND TEN, 2026-08-17. Design:
# docs/specs/2026-08-17-battle-chronicles-cases-design.md
#
# WHY MORE TEMPLATES DOES NOT MEAN MORE INTERRUPTIONS. The rate limiter is
# bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger, not the weights - this file's own header
# says so and the code agrees. A commander who has just taken a moment makes
# EVERY event in this file invalid for the next bc_chronicle_flag_days_value
# days, so a blocked roll is not a Battle Chronicles roll at all; it goes to
# vanilla's commander_none. Adding templates therefore mostly changes WHICH
# moment a battle produces, not how many.
#
# IT IS NOT PURELY FREE, AND THE HONEST HALF IS THIS. Six of the original seven
# carry a situational gate, so an even battle on open ground with an unremarkable
# commander currently matches NOTHING and passes in silence. Those battles will
# now speak. That is an accepted, deliberate rate increase in the one place the
# mod was mute, and it is still capped by the same cooldown.
#
# BASE 30 IS THE DEFAULT HERE, against 40-100 for the original seven. `chance` is
# a weight against every other VALID event, including commander_none at
# base = 1000, so ten more templates at the old bases would roughly triple the
# mod's share of the pool. Thirty keeps the calibrated seven dominant among BC
# events while still letting the new ones appear. Two exceptions, both argued at
# their event.
#
# NO POP-UPS IN THIS BATCH. A pop-up spends bc_popup_cooldown, and the three
# shipped ones already compete with the eve for it. These are toasts only, which
# is also why none of them branches on bc_can_interrupt_player_trigger.
#
# NO NEW MODIFIERS AND NO NEW GAME RULE. Every one of these routes through the
# existing (KIND, SWING) table in bc_apply_verb_effect and the existing
# momentum/faltering pairs.
#
# STACKING, THE RULE THAT DECIDES EVERY is_valid BELOW: a template that grants
# momentum gates `NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes }`, one that grants
# faltering gates `NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }`, and one that grants
# neither needs neither. bc_faltering_minor and bc_faltering_major are different
# modifier keys, so two live at once runs off the end of the ladder the mod is
# tuned against.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Winter bites. The first event in the mod to gate on a GROUND BAND rather than
# on the older favours/bogs pair - and the bands are the same six the prose and
# the reputation streaks use, so a winter battle now reads as winter in three
# places at once.
#
# base = 40, above the batch default: the winter band was 38 of 360 classified
# fires in the 2026-08-17 session, so the gate is genuinely narrow.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_winter_bites = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }
		bc_field_is_winter_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_winter_bites"

		bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = bc_winter_bites_toast
			desc = bc_winter_bites_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dust and thirst. The desert band.
#
# THE DESERT BAND SCORED ZERO in both terrain censuses taken so far - the DIAG
# run's Q5 (0 of 137) and the 2026-08-17 session (0 of 360). That is a statement
# about where these campaigns were fought, not about the map: the band holds
# desert, desert_mountains, oasis and canyon, and Dorne and the eastern
# continent are made of them. This is deliberately a row that ages into
# relevance, the same argument bc_battle_is_notable_trigger's 20,000 row makes.
# Do not delete it for scoring zero on a Westerlands campaign.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_dust_and_thirst = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }
		bc_field_is_desert_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_dust_and_thirst"

		bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = bc_dust_and_thirst_toast
			desc = bc_dust_and_thirst_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The woods hide us. Forest ground read by the WEAKER side.
#
# THE OUTNUMBERED ROW IS WHAT MAKES THIS A NEW CASE RATHER THAN A THEFT.
# bc_ground_favours_us already fires on forest - forest is in its favouring
# list - so without that row this would be a strict subset of a calibrated
# event and would only take weight from it. Being outnumbered in woods is the
# distinct situation: the trees are worth more to the side with fewer men.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_woods_hide_us = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes }
		bc_field_is_forest_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Using ground is a martial skill, the same read bc_ground_favours_us
		# makes and at the same bar.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_woods_hide_us"

		bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_woods_hide_us_toast
			desc = bc_the_woods_hide_us_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Too wide to see. Fifteen thousand men on one field, counting both hosts.
#
# num_total_troops is a TRIGGER ONLY - it cannot be read into a variable, which
# is the trap bc_battle_open_effect's header documents at length. Asking it here,
# in a gate, is the one context where that limitation costs nothing.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_too_wide_to_see = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }
		bc_host_is_vast_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A great captain keeps hold of a great host; a poor one loses it.
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			martial <= 8
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_too_wide_to_see"

		bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = bc_too_wide_to_see_toast
			desc = bc_too_wide_to_see_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The killing is general. Two fifths of the enemy already dead, asked while the
# number is still climbing.
#
# THE BAR IS 40, NOT THE NOTABILITY TRIGGER'S 50, and the difference is not an
# oversight. That row grades a FINISHED battle; this one is asked every five
# days from the middle of one, so the same number would be a strictly harder
# test. See bc_field_is_bloody_trigger.
#
# NAMED OFF `field` DELIBERATELY. The first draft was bc_the_field_is_a_slaughter
# and the word is spoken for: bc_frag_bond_nemesis and bc_frag_bond_kin both
# carry `field`, and a bond fragment renders in the sentence IMMEDIATELY AFTER
# this toast's literal on every fire. The loc file's curated shortlist is the
# list to check a new toast against, and it is the reason two events in this
# batch were renamed before their prose was written rather than after.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_killing_is_general = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes }
		bc_field_is_bloody_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_killing_is_general"

		bc_momentum_major_effect = yes
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = major KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_killing_is_general_toast
			desc = bc_the_killing_is_general_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Kin across the line. The foe as a person, in a GATE - the bond ladder has
# known this since Plan A but only ever spent it on prose.
#
# ITS BOND FRAGMENT IS NOT A FREE SECOND SENTENCE, IT IS A CONSTRAINT. This gate
# is bc_foe_is_kin_trigger and the bond ladder's `kin` arm is the same
# is_close_family_of test, so whenever this toast fires the bond clause after it
# resolves to bc_frag_bond_kin or - if the kinsman is also the nemesis, which the
# ladder ranks first - bc_frag_bond_nemesis. Either way the relation is ALREADY
# STATED in the following sentence. The literal must therefore not restate it;
# it says what the relation costs him instead.
#
# THE SAME is_close_family_of THE BOND LADDER USES, through
# bc_foe_is_kin_trigger, so a battle gated as a kinslaying cannot then have its
# toast call the man a stranger.
#
# base = 50: the second exception to the batch default. Close family across a
# battle line is rare enough that at 30 it would be a template nobody ever sees,
# and it is the most characterful of the ten when it does happen.
#
# NO MOMENTUM EITHER WAY, so it needs no stacking gate. This is a moment about
# the man, not about the battle - the only mechanical trace is stress.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_kin_across_the_line = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_foe_is_kin_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who feels things feels this one harder.
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_kin_across_the_line"

		add_stress = 15
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = bc_kin_across_the_line_toast
			desc = bc_kin_across_the_line_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The green boy. Age, downward. The mirror of the one above, and the pair is

			factor = 2

		}

			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = brave

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# His lord's war. The commander is not the man whose quarrel this is.
#
# THE WIDEST GATE OF THE TEN, and knowingly so - most commanders in the world are
# not war leaders. Two rows narrow it: the side must be winning, and momentum
# must be free. It stays at base = 30 for that reason and should be the first
# weight re-examined if the next sweep shows this batch crowding the original
# seven.
#
# THE TRIGGER MAKES NO CLAIM THAT THE LORD IS PRESENT. side_primary_participant
# is the war leader of the side and he may be a kingdom away; the prose is
# written about fighting another man's war, not about being watched. Do not
# rewrite it the other way - nothing available here could support that.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_his_lords_war = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes }
		bc_fighting_his_lords_war_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A dutiful man makes more of the obligation.
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_his_lords_war"

		bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		add_prestige = 25
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_his_lords_war_toast
			desc = bc_his_lords_war_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE THIRD BATCH: WHO THE COMMANDER IS (2026-08-17)
#
# Six templates on the ONE AXIS THE OTHER TWENTY DO NOT GATE ON - the man's
# character. Every shipped template asks about the battle: the ground, the odds,
# the dead, the war, his age, whose quarrel it is. `has_trait` appears in this
# file eleven times and every one of them is a `chance` modifier, never a row in
# an is_valid. So two commanders in the same battle have always been able to
# produce only the same set of moments, which is the wrong answer for a mod
# whose whole output is character.
#
# EVERY GATE HERE IS A TRAIT PLUS A SITUATION, never a trait alone. A bare
# `has_trait = wrathful` is valid in EVERY battle that man ever fights, which
# makes the template a property of the commander instead of a moment in a fight
# - and it would crowd out the calibrated seven for exactly the characters most
# likely to be somebody's player. The second row also gives the prose something
# to describe. bc_the_wine_at_noon is the single exception and says why at its
# own comment.
#
# ALL SIX AT base = 30, the second batch's default, for the second batch's
# reason. The running total is the thing to watch and it is written down here so
# the next reader does not have to add it up: seven calibrated templates at
# 40-100 (~400), ten at 30 (300), six more at 30 (180), against vanilla's
# commander_none at base = 1000. If a sweep shows the original seven crowded
# out, THIS BATCH IS THE FIRST TO CUT - it is the newest and the least measured.
#
# ALL SIX TRAIT KEYS ARE AGOT KEYS, checked in AGOT's own common/traits/00_traits.txt
# and not in vanilla's: wrathful :2821, patient :3074, drunkard :5437,
# zealous :4423, craven :3668, strategist :1543. AGOT overrides that file
# wholesale, so a key that exists only in vanilla is not a key at all here.
#
# NO TRAIT IS AWARDED OR REMOVED BY ANY OF THEM. These READ character; the
# reputation chain in bc_reputation_effects.txt is the only thing in the mod
# that writes it, and it does so off the style streak. A template that both
# gated on a trait and granted one would be a feedback loop.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The wrath takes him. Wrathful, and the day is going badly.
#
# THE SECOND ROW IS `losing`, NOT `winning`, and the pairing is the case. Anger
# in a won battle is just a man enjoying himself; anger in a lost one is the
# thing that either saves it or finishes it. This mod grants it momentum, which
# is the generous reading, and the swing is `up` for that reason.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_wrath_takes_him = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes }
		has_trait = wrathful
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Anger and courage compound; anger and fear do not.
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			has_trait = brave
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_wrath_takes_him"

		bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_wrath_takes_him_toast
			desc = bc_the_wrath_takes_him_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The patient hand. Patient, and outnumbered.

# SHARES THE `outnumbered` ROW WITH bc_the_woods_hide_us AND IS NOT A SUBSET OF
# IT - that one also requires the forest band, this one requires a trait
# instead. Neither gate contains the other, which is the distinctness rule the
# design asks of every template.

		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes

			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Waiting is easier for a man who was taught to.
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			has_trait = education_martial_4
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# else has gone wrong. It is also the narrowest gate of the six - drunkard is a

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The zealot will not stop. Zealous, with two fifths of the enemy already down.
#
# SHARES bc_field_is_bloody_trigger WITH bc_the_killing_is_general, which grants
# MAJOR momentum off the same row. This one is minor and gates on the trait
# instead of on the momentum-free state alone, so on a battle where both are
# valid the bigger event is also the better-weighted one and this reads as the
# consolation. That ordering is intended.

bc_the_zealot_will_not_stop = {

			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He looks for the road. Craven, and the side is losing.
#
# THE ONLY COMMANDER TEMPLATE THAT GATES ON craven. bc_knight_shames_himself
# reads the same trait but is `type = knight`, roots on a knight, and competes
# in a different pool against knight_none - so the two never contend and the
# commander half of this idea was simply missing.
#
# KIND = measured, WHICH IS NOT AN ACCIDENT AND IS NOT FLATTERY. The style
# streak turns three of these into cautious_leader, and a man who keeps looking
# for the road is a cautious commander whatever the reason. The mod does not
# have a cowardice trait to award and inventing one to be unkind with is not
# worth a new trait file.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_he_looks_for_the_road = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }
		has_trait = craven
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Being badly outnumbered on top of it settles the argument.
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_he_looks_for_the_road"

		bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		add_stress = 10
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = bc_he_looks_for_the_road_toast
			desc = bc_he_looks_for_the_road_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The practised eye. A strategist on a host too big to see the end of.
#
# strategist IS A LIFESTYLE TRAIT AND THAT IS WHY IT IS HERE. The other five
# gates in this batch read personality, which a man is born with; this one reads
# something he went and learned. It also pairs with bc_too_wide_to_see off the
# same bc_host_is_vast_trigger and answers it: the same battle that loses an
# ordinary commander is the one a trained one is for.

bc_the_practised_eye = {

		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes

		}

			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 16
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_practised_eye"

			desc = bc_the_practised_eye_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# BATCH 2026-08-17, THE FOURTH: THE LAST TWO BANDS
#
# CLOSING THE GROUND SET, NOT OPENING A NEW AXIS. Three earlier templates gate
# on a band - bc_winter_bites, bc_dust_and_thirst, bc_the_woods_hide_us - and
# bc_scripted_triggers.txt carried a note that only four of the six bands had a
# combat-side wrapper because only four were gated on. The two below are the
# reason the other two now exist. Every band the prose can name, an event can
# name; a reader who sees "rough" in the eve text will not wonder why nothing
# in the battle ever reads it.
#
# NEITHER IS A SUBSET OF AN EXISTING GATE, the rule that killed the obvious
# first draft of the rough one. Rough plus bc_host_is_vast_trigger would have
# been a strict subset of bc_too_wide_to_see, which gates on the vast host
# ALONE and grants faltering - so it could only have taken weight from a
# calibrated event. Rough plus losing is instead the answer to bc_banner_falls,
# the same shape bc_the_wrath_takes_him has against the same pair: same
# situation, opposite grant, so the two compete rather than nest.
#
# WEIGHT. bc_the_broken_ground_holds is 30, the batch default, because rough is
# the widest band by member count and the gate is not narrow. bc_the_green_
# closes_in is 40, matching bc_winter_bites and bc_dust_and_thirst, because a
# band that only Sothoryos and the Summer Isles can produce needs the help on
# the rare occasion it is asked at all. Running total against commander_none's
# 1000 is now roughly 400 + 300 + 180 + 70; this batch is still cheap and is
# still not the first thing to cut - that remains the character batch.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The broken ground holds. Losing, on hills or bog or mountain, and the ground
# is what stops the rout.
#
# THE ANSWER TO bc_banner_falls. That template reads the same losing side and
# grants faltering; this one says the ground gave the line something to stand
# behind. Both are valid on the same roll and the pick decides which story the
# battle tells, which is the intended behaviour of the weight system and not a
# collision.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_broken_ground_holds = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes }
		bc_field_is_rough_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Reading ground is a martial skill, the same bar bc_the_woods_hide_us
		# and bc_ground_favours_us use.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_broken_ground_holds"

		bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_broken_ground_holds_toast
			desc = bc_the_broken_ground_holds_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The green closes in. The jungle band, which is the sixth and last.
#
# JUNGLE IS RARE BUT NOT ZERO, and the number is measured: the DIAG run's Q5
# terrain census read jungle 8 of 137 against rough 58 and desert 0. So unlike
# bc_dust_and_thirst this is not a row waiting on a campaign in another
# continent - it is a row that will fire a few times in an ordinary game, which
# is precisely the frequency a distinctive line wants.
#
# GATED ON THE BAND ALONE, no second condition, because eight in a hundred and
# thirty-seven with a second gate on top is a row that never fires at all
# rather than a row that fires seldom.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_green_closes_in = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }
		bc_field_is_jungle_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_green_closes_in"

		bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = bc_the_green_closes_in_toast
			desc = bc_the_green_closes_in_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# BATCH 2026-08-17, THE FIFTH: WHO IS ACROSS THE FIELD
#
# THE BOND LADDER HAS FOUR ANSWERS AND ONE OF THEM HAD A MOMENT. Every toast in
# the mod ends with a clause naming the foe as a nemesis, kin, a countryman or a
# heathen - bc_write_bond_effect has classified all four since the prose work -
# but only kin was ever a REASON for a moment to fire (bc_kin_across_the_line).
# Nemesis was a `chance` modifier on nineteen templates and a gate on none. This
# batch makes the other three gates, which is the last cheap axis the commander
# side has: no new state, no new modifier, and two new triggers that read
# exactly what the ladder reads.
#
# THE GATES OVERLAP AND THE CLAUSE MAY DISAGREE WITH THEM. bc_foe_is_countryman
# _trigger and bc_foe_is_heathen_trigger each test one property, while the
# ladder tests culture before faith and stops - so a same-culture, different-
# faith foe passes both gates here and is called `countryman` in the clause
# under the toast. That is not a defect to fix in either place: a gate asks one
# question and a clause reports one classification. See the header on the two
# triggers in bc_scripted_triggers.txt.
#
# ALL THREE AT base = 30, the batch default, and each grants a different thing,
# which is what keeps them from being three copies of one template.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The old grudge. bc_facing_nemesis_trigger AS A GATE for the first time.
#
# IT WAS ALREADY IN NINETEEN chance BLOCKS at factor 1.5, and a weight is not a
# case: it makes every other moment slightly likelier against a man you hate,
# and it never once produces a moment that is ABOUT hating him. This is that
# moment, and it is the reason the trigger was written.
#
# NO SECOND ROW. The gate is already the narrowest in the file - it needs a live
# nemesis relation, recorded by bc_rivalry_effect on some earlier battle, AND
# that man commanding the other side today.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_old_grudge = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes }
		bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# NO bc_facing_nemesis_trigger ROW HERE, unlike nineteen other
		# templates. It is the gate; a factor on it would be a factor of one.
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			has_trait = wrathful
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = forgiving
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_old_grudge"

		bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		add_stress = 10
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_old_grudge_toast
			desc = bc_the_old_grudge_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Gods on both sides. The foe holds another faith.
#
# THE ONLY COMMANDER TEMPLATE THAT PAYS IN PIETY, matching the eve's option h,
# which is the only OPTION that does. The pairing is deliberate: a man who
# preached the war on the eve and a man who finds his priests useful in the
# middle of it are the same story told twice, and a player who takes h and then
# reads this has had the same idea the mod had.
#
# THE ZEALOT ROW IS A WEIGHT AND NOT A GATE, unlike bc_the_zealot_will_not_stop,
# which needs the trait. A man who does not care what they pray to still gets
# the moment; he just gets it less often.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_gods_on_both_sides = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes }
		bc_foe_is_heathen_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			has_trait = zealous
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = cynical
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_gods_on_both_sides"

		bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		add_piety = 50
		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_gods_on_both_sides_toast
			desc = bc_gods_on_both_sides_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The same tongue. The foe is of his own culture.
#
# NO RUNG AND THEREFORE NO STACKING GUARD, the third template in the mod built
# that way and for the reason the other two were: bc_kin_across_the_line and
# bc_knight_meets_his_cousin both say that recognising the other side is a thing
# that happens to a man rather than a thing that moves a battle. Hearing your
# own words shouted back is the weakest of the three recognitions, so it is the
# one that costs the least - five stress against their ten and fifteen.

bc_the_same_tongue = {

			factor = 0.5

			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = callous

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes

# ===========================================================================
# BATCH 2026-08-17, THE SIXTH ON THIS SIDE: WHAT HE HAS TO LOSE
#
# TWENTY-EIGHT COMMANDER TEMPLATES AND NOT ONE READS HIS STATION. The mod asks
# what the battle is doing (twelve), where it is happening (six), who is
# opposite (four), how old he is and what he is like (eight) - and never what
# he is worth. A king and a landless sworn sword get exactly the same
# chronicle, and they are not having the same day.
#
# THE THREE ARE ONE QUESTION WITH THREE ANSWERS: what does this man stand to
# lose if the line goes? A kingdom, nothing at all, or somebody. That is the
# reason they ship together rather than as three unrelated rows.
#
# `highest_held_title_tier` AND `is_landed` ARE ENGINE TRIGGERS, both confirmed
# in ../../docs/api/1.19.0.6/triggers with `Supported Scopes: character`, and
# `tier_kingdom` is a vanilla script value from
# common/script_values/00_title_tiers_values.txt:5. Vanilla writes the exact
# comparison at events/activities/chariot_race_activity/
# chariot_race_ongoing_events.txt:3083. NONE of these is AGOT-overridden the way
# the trait file is, so an engine-dump confirmation is sufficient evidence here
# in a way it is not for a trait key.
#
# NONE OF THE THREE IS A SUBSET of a shipped gate, and the station rows make
# that easy: no `is_valid` anywhere in the mod reads a title tier, `is_landed`
# or the side's own knight list, so only the second rows need checking. The
# crown case pairs with losing, where the shipped gates are bc_banner_falls
# (momentum-free), bc_rally_the_line, bc_flank_collapses, bc_the_broken_ground_
# holds (rough) and bc_he_looks_for_the_road (craven); a landed king who is
# none of those things matches only this one.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The crown on a losing field. He is the highest thing standing on it and
# everybody on both sides knows exactly where he is.
#
# LOSING IS THE SECOND ROW AND IT IS ALSO WHAT MAKES THE MOMENT. A king on a
# winning field is a man being carried along by his own army. A king on a
# losing one is a target with a war behind him, and the difference is the
# whole story.
#
# IT ALSO NARROWS A GATE THAT WOULD OTHERWISE FIRE FOR A PLAYER-KING IN EVERY
# BATTLE HE COMMANDS. The cooldown is the real rate limiter - see this file's
# header - but a template that matches a particular player on every single roll
# would still crowd his chronicle with one sentence, which is the problem the
# whole content programme exists to solve.
#
# `bloodied`/`down` AND A GUARDED FALTERING RUNG. The side pays for where he is
# standing: men come off the line to get between him and it, which is
# bc_bloodied's trade made by somebody else's decision.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_crown_on_the_field = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# An emperor is a bigger thing to be standing in a field.
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_empire
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_crown_on_the_field"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_crown_on_the_field_toast
			desc = bc_the_crown_on_the_field_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The other end of the same question. `is_landed = no`: he commands and he
# holds nothing, so a lost battle costs him a horse and a lost war costs him
# nothing he owns.
#
# NO SECOND ROW, DELIBERATELY. The crown case needed one because it would
# otherwise own a player-king's chronicle; this one does not, because a
# landless commander is already the minority case - most men trusted with a
# wing hold something - and because the moment is true whichever way the
# battle is going. That asymmetry inside one batch is intended.
#
# `is_landed = no`, NOT `is_landless_adventurer`. The second is not in the
# 1.19.0.6 trigger dump at all, so it is either a mod's or does not exist, and
# a misspelled trigger key is not the kind of thing that fails loudly.
#
# `wild`/`up` AND A GUARDED MOMENTUM RUNG - the tide moves and the horses pay
# for it. A man with nothing behind him rides at the thing rather than round
# it, which is bc_spent_horse exactly. bc_the_wrath_takes_him and
# bc_the_old_grudge are the two shipped templates that already mean this, and
# both reach it from temperament rather than from station.
#
# HE IS PAID IN PRESTIGE, which is not decoration. Prestige is the only thing
# a landless man is actually accumulating out here.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_nothing_but_the_horse = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		is_landed = no
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Ambition is the whole engine of this one.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_nothing_but_the_horse"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_nothing_but_the_horse_toast
			desc = bc_nothing_but_the_horse_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The third answer: somebody. His own close family is in the knight list on his
# own side, and he has been keeping track of one man in a fight that needs all
# of him.
#
# THE MOD HAS READ HIS OWN SIDE'S KNIGHTS EXACTLY ONCE before this -
# bc_shieldbrother_forged, which only counts them. bc_side_has_kin_knight_
# trigger is the first gate that asks WHO they are, and it uses the bond
# ladder's own `is_close_family_of`, pointed inward instead of across.
#
# IT IS NOT bc_kin_across_the_line WITH THE SIGN FLIPPED. That one is about a
# man he will have to explain at a funeral; this one is about a man he is
# trying to keep out of one, and the two consequences are opposite. The foe
# case is `measured`/`down` and grants nothing - recognising the other side
# does not move a battle. This is `bloodied`/`up` with a rung, because a man
# fighting where his own blood can see him fights harder and worse.
#
# base = 40, between the batch's 30 and bc_kin_across_the_line's 50. Close
# family among your own knights is commoner than close family commanding the
# other side and rarer than the plain gates.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_his_blood_in_the_ranks = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_side_has_kin_knight_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = callous
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_his_blood_in_the_ranks"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_his_blood_in_the_ranks_toast
			desc = bc_his_blood_in_the_ranks_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# BATCH 2026-08-17, THE SEVENTH ON THIS SIDE: WHAT THE MEN THINK OF HIM
#
# `dread` APPEARS NOWHERE IN THE MOD. Not in a gate, not in a `chance` row, not
# in a scripted trigger - `grep -c dread` over both phase event files returned
# zero before this batch. It is the largest single character axis the mod had
# never read, and it is the one that is *about* the relationship between a
# commander and the men in front of him, which is the only relationship a
# battle actually consists of.
#
# TEMPERAMENT IS NOT DREAD AND THE EIGHT TRAIT TEMPLATES DO NOT COVER THIS.
# `has_trait = arrogant` is what he is like. Dread is what has been done to
# people, accumulated over a reign, and a mild man who hanged a town has it
# while a loud one who never did anything does not. The mod has asked what he
# is like eight times and never once what he is *feared* to be.
#
# THE THRESHOLDS ARE VANILLA NAMED VALUES, NOT LITERALS. `low_dread` (20),
# `medium_dread` (40) and `major_dread_value` (70) are defined in vanilla's
# common/script_values/00_basic_values.txt:993, :994 and :739. AGOT OVERRIDES
# THAT FILE and redefines all three, so this was checked rather than assumed:
# AGOT's 00_basic_values.txt:1005, :1006 and :751 carry 20, 40 and 70 - the
# same numbers. Using the names rather than the numbers means the gates follow
# whichever file wins the load order.
#
# NONE OF THE THREE IS A SUBSET of a shipped gate, and no shipped `is_valid`
# reads dread at all, so only the second rows need the argument. Two of them
# pair with losing - where the shipped gates are bc_banner_falls,
# bc_rally_the_line, bc_flank_collapses, bc_the_broken_ground_holds (rough),
# bc_he_looks_for_the_road (craven) and bc_the_crown_on_the_field (a crown) -
# and a feared or unfeared commander who is none of those things matches only
# these. THE TWO LOSING CASES ARE DISJOINT FROM EACH OTHER: `>= medium_dread`
# is 40 and up, `<= low_dread` is 20 and down, and no character is both.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The line holds because going backwards means answering to him afterwards.
#
# THIS IS THE ONE THING DREAD IS ACTUALLY FOR. Vanilla spends it on vassals
# not revolting; here it is spent on the same men not running, which is the
# same fact standing in a field.
#
# LOSING IS THE SECOND ROW AND IT IS WHAT MAKES THE MOMENT. A feared commander
# on a winning field is a man whose army was going to hold anyway. The fear
# only shows up when there is a reason to run and nobody takes it.
#
# `entrenched`/`up` AND A GUARDED MOMENTUM RUNG. A losing side that does not
# come apart when it should have is a real turn, and bc_shieldwall - feet set,
# nothing getting through and nothing getting done - is exactly what this looks
# like from outside. The rung is guarded because a side already carrying
# momentum does not get a second helping; same rule as every other grant.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_they_fear_me_more = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		dread >= medium_dread
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Past the point where it is a reputation and into where it is a fact.
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			dread >= major_dread_value
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_they_fear_me_more"

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_they_fear_me_more_toast
			desc = bc_they_fear_me_more_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The other end of the same measure. Nothing is keeping them but their own
# reading of the day, and their own reading of the day is that it is lost.
#
# `<= low_dread` IS NOT "HE IS KIND". A character who has simply never done
# anything sits here alongside one who is loved, and the template says nothing
# about which - it says only that there is no fear in the ranks to spend.
#
# `wild`/`down` AND A GUARDED FALTERING RUNG. What goes wrong here is not the
# line bracing badly, it is the line coming apart in pieces, each piece
# deciding for itself - which is bc_spent_horse's disorder rather than
# bc_bloodied's price. The mod's other reading of the same shape is
# bc_flank_collapses, and that one is decisive where this is minor.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_no_fear_in_them = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		dread <= low_dread
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A commander with nothing to hold them and no name to hold them with.
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			has_trait = craven
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			martial >= 14
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_no_fear_in_them"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_no_fear_in_them_toast
			desc = bc_no_fear_in_them_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The third answer, and the only one that is not about whether the line holds.
# At `>= major_dread_value` he does not have to argue with anyone. He sends
# them where they will not come back from and they go, and the battle gets the
# ground he wanted at the price he was always going to pay.
#
# NO SIDE ROW, DELIBERATELY. The other two are about a side that is losing;
# this one is about how the orders are given, and they are given the same way
# on a good day. bc_nothing_but_the_horse is the shipped precedent for a
# commander gate with no second row.
#
# NO RUNG AT ALL. Both rungs are about the side's standing, and this template
# does not claim the day moved - it claims the day cost. bc_knight_will_not_be_
# carried is the shipped precedent for a template that records a moment and
# grants nothing.
#
# HE IS PAID IN PRESTIGE AND HE PAYS IN STRESS, because both are true at once:
# the field respects it and he watched it.
#
# `bloodied`/`up` IS THE POINT OF THE WHOLE TEMPLATE - ground bought with men,
# and bought on purpose.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_hard_hand = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		dread >= major_dread_value
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			has_trait = callous
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_hard_hand"

		add_prestige = 50
		add_stress = 10

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_hard_hand_toast
			desc = bc_the_hard_hand_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# BATCH 2026-08-17, THE EIGHTH ON THIS SIDE: WHAT HE IS ALREADY CARRYING
#
# THE MOD WRITES STRESS THIRTY-FOUR TIMES AND HAS NEVER ONCE READ IT BACK.
# `add_stress` appears eleven times in this file, eleven in the knight file,
# nine across the pop-ups and three in the eve - and `stress_level` appears
# nowhere at all. Every one of those writes is the mod saying a battle costs a
# man something; not one of them has ever been allowed to matter afterwards.
#
# STRESS IS NOT TEMPERAMENT AND THE EIGHT TRAIT TEMPLATES DO NOT COVER IT.
# `has_trait = craven` is a man who has always been like this. Stress level 2 is
# a man who was fine in the spring, and the difference is the only thing on the
# field that this mod itself has been putting there.
#
# `stress_level` IS AN ENGINE TRIGGER, confirmed in ../../docs/api/1.19.0.6/
# triggers with the comparison traits and `Supported Scopes: character`, and
# AGOT writes the exact comparison this batch uses at events/activities/
# tournaments/ep2_locale_events.txt:5397, where its own comment glosses
# `stress_level >= 2` as "the second breaking point of stress".
#
# THE TWO COMMANDER CASES ARE DISJOINT: one pairs with losing and one with
# winning, so they can never compete for the same roll. No shipped `is_valid`
# reads stress at all, so nothing else here needs the subset argument.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He is at the second breaking point and the day is going against him.
#
# WHAT GOES WRONG IS THE ORDERS, NOT THE MAN'S COURAGE. He is still standing
# where he should be. What has stopped working is the part of him that decides
# what to do next, and a battle is nothing but a series of those decisions
# arriving faster than they can be made.
#
# `measured`/`down` GRANTS NOTHING, AND HERE THAT IS THE WHOLE POINT. There is
# no army modifier that means "the man in charge is misreading it" - the army
# is doing exactly what it was told and what it was told was wrong. The
# faltering rung is the consequence and the ledger entry is the record; no verb
# is the honest third answer.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_hands_are_not_steady = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		stress_level >= 2
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Past the second breaking point and into the third.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			stress_level >= 3
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			martial >= 14
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_hands_are_not_steady"

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_hands_are_not_steady_toast
			desc = bc_the_hands_are_not_steady_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The same man on a day that is going his way, which does not help him.
#
# HE CANNOT STOP. The side is winning and he keeps spending it - one more push,
# and then one more, past the point where anybody would have called it enough.
# That is not courage and the prose must not write it as courage; it is a man
# who cannot put a thing down.
#
# `wild`/`up` AND A GUARDED MOMENTUM RUNG. The tide moves and the horses pay
# for the moving, which is bc_spent_horse exactly. bc_the_wrath_takes_him and
# bc_the_old_grudge are the shipped templates that mean this; both reach it
# from temperament, and this one reaches it from a number the mod put there
# itself.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_he_cannot_stop = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		stress_level >= 2
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			stress_level >= 3
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_he_cannot_stop"

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
