			desc = bc_he_cannot_stop_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# WHERE HE LEARNED IT, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE EDUCATION AXIS APPEARED ONCE IN THE WHOLE MOD, in a comment, and never
# in a gate. Thirty-four commander templates read martial, prowess, twelve
# temperament traits, dread, stress, station, age and the health traits, and
# not one of them asked what the man was RAISED to do. In Westeros that is not
# a footnote: a second son sent to the yard and a second son sent to the
# Citadel both end up commanding when the war reaches them.
#
# EDUCATION IS NOT MARTIAL AND THE TWO MUST NOT BE COLLAPSED. martial is how
# good he is at it now, and a lifetime of lifestyle xp, a genius trait or a
# congenital roll can carry a man to 16 who was never taught a thing. The
# education trait is where he STARTED, which is a different sentence and the
# one nothing here has ever said.
#
# THE TRAIT KEYS ARE LISTED, NOT GROUPED. `has_trait = education_martial`
# would be shorter and it is not written that way on purpose: AGOT's override
# of common/traits/00_traits.txt defines education_martial_1 at :480, _2 at
# :524, _3 at :561 and _4 at :599 with `category = education` and no `group`
# line, so there is no group key to test. Listing the four is the only form
# that is certain to hold in both files.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man who was put in a yard at seven and is now doing the thing he was put
# there for.
#
# `education_martial_4` IS THE TOP RUNG AND IS DELIBERATELY NOT WIDENED to
# include _3. The case is not "had some training" - roughly half the lords in
# a Westerosi host would clear that - it is the man for whom this day is the
# purpose of his entire upbringing. One rung keeps it rare enough to mean
# something when the toast appears.
#
# WINNING, BECAUSE THE POINT IS THAT IT SHOWS. A losing field would make this
# a sentence about how the training was not enough, which is a different
# template and a worse one - bc_never_learned_the_trade below is the one that
# owns being out of your depth.
#
# `ordered`/`up` AND A GUARDED MOMENTUM RUNG. bc_pursuit is what the seam
# hands out for ordered/up: a host doing the correct thing at the correct
# moment gets to keep going. The shipped templates on that cell reach it from
# discipline in the moment; this one reaches it from twenty years earlier.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_taught_to_do_this = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_trait = education_martial_4
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = strategist
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_taught_to_do_this"

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_taught_to_do_this_toast
			desc = bc_taught_to_do_this_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The other end of the same axis: the man who was raised to do something else
# entirely and is holding a host because the war reached his gate.
#
# `NOR` OVER THE FOUR MARTIAL KEYS, NOT `education_martial_1`. Level 1 is a
# bad soldier; this is a man who is not a soldier at all - the Citadel-taught
# heir, the diplomat, the schemer. That is the AGOT case worth writing and it
# is much commoner than a martial_1, which the education system hands out
# rarely.
#
# IT CANNOT COLLIDE WITH bc_taught_to_do_this. The two gates are complements
# on the same four keys and additionally take opposite side rows, so no
# battle state can satisfy both.
#
# `measured`/`down`, WHICH GRANTS NOTHING. The man is not doing anything
# wrong; he is doing the careful, sensible, wrong-shaped thing a clever man
# does when the problem is not the kind of problem he knows. Handing him a
# modifier would be the template arguing that being out of your depth has a
# mechanical texture, and it does not - the faltering rung is the whole cost.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_never_learned_the_trade = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		NOR = {
			has_trait = education_martial_1
			has_trait = education_martial_2
			has_trait = education_martial_3
			has_trait = education_martial_4
		}
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial <= 8
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			martial >= 14
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_never_learned_the_trade"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_never_learned_the_trade_toast
			desc = bc_never_learned_the_trade_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE SAME NAME ON BOTH SIDES, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE MOD READ KIN AND STOPPED THERE. `is_close_family_of` appears sixteen
# times - parent, child, sibling, on both the commander and the knight side -
# and the house never appeared at all. In Westeros that is the wrong place to
# stop: the wars that define the setting are fought BETWEEN branches of the
# same name far more often than between brothers, and every one of those men
# is carrying a shield the other one recognises.
#
# THE GATE IS bc_foe_is_house_trigger AND IT IS DISJOINT FROM THE KIN GATE by
# construction - its own header records why and how. So these two templates
# cannot collide with bc_kin_across_the_line or bc_knight_meets_his_cousin,
# and the four together cover the two cases separately rather than the wider
# one swallowing the narrower.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He has won it, and he is not going to finish it.
#
# THE WINNING SIDE IS THE POINT. A man losing to his own house has no decision
# to make; a man WINNING against it has exactly one, and he makes it by
# omission - the line stops at the top of the field and nobody gives the order
# to go on. The men notice. Nobody says anything.
#
# `ordered`/`down`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING, AND THAT IS THE WHOLE MECHANIC.
# The seam's table gives bc_pursuit to `ordered`/`up` and deliberately gives
# nothing to `ordered`/`down` - a formation that keeps its shape while it
# declines to press. That cell was written for a pursuit that walks into trees
# and stops being one, and this is the same shape reached for a reason a man
# chose. He keeps his order and forgoes the pursuit modifier: the cost of the
# decision is the modifier he does not get.
#
# NO RUNG IN EITHER DIRECTION. The side is winning and stays winning - nothing
# about the battle's standing changed, only what was done with it.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_he_will_not_chase_them = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_foe_is_house_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = callous
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_he_will_not_chase_them"

		add_stress = 10

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = bc_he_will_not_chase_them_toast
			desc = bc_he_will_not_chase_them_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE STATE OF THE CHEST, 2026-08-17.
#
# `gold` IS READ TWENTY-NINE TIMES IN THIS MOD AND EVERY ONE OF THEM IS A
# PAYMENT OR A GATE ON A PAYMENT - the eve's `gold >= 100`, the pop-up
# purchases, the two knight rewards. Not one template has ever asked what
# state the man's finances are in as a REASON for anything, and a host is the
# most expensive thing a lord ever owns. Wages, carts, fodder and the smith
# are all on the same ledger as the war.
#
# DEBT IS NOT GREED AND NOT POVERTY OF BIRTH. `greedy` is a temperament the
# mod already reads twice, `is_lowborn` is where a man started, and this is a
# number that was fine last year. The three are independent: a greedy highborn
# lord can be four hundred in the hole and a content hedge knight can be
# solvent.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The men have not been paid, and today is the day it starts to matter.
#
# `gold < 0` IS THE HONEST TEST FOR THIS. CK3 lets a character run negative
# and that is precisely the state being written: not a poor man, a man whose
# obligations have already outrun what he has. A threshold like `gold < 100`
# would catch every landless knight in Westeros and say nothing.
#
# LOSING, BECAUSE UNPAID MEN WINNING IS NOT A STORY. A host that is carrying
# the day does not stop to count what it is owed; a host going backwards for a
# man who has not paid it since the spring does that arithmetic immediately,
# and it is the arithmetic and not the debt that is the moment.
#
# `wild`/`down` AND A GUARDED FALTERING RUNG. bc_spent_horse is what the seam
# hands out for wild: things are being spent that will not come back. What is
# being spent here is not the horses' wind but the reason the men are standing
# there at all, and the modifier is the same shape - a cost taken now that is
# felt afterwards.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_chest_is_empty = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		gold < 0
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			gold < -100
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_war_is_long_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			dread >= major_dread_value
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_chest_is_empty"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_chest_is_empty_toast
			desc = bc_the_chest_is_empty_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE GODS, WHICH THE MOD HAD ONLY EVER READ AS A TEMPERAMENT.
#
# `zealous` appears as a gate once and as a chance row several times, and
# `piety_level` appeared nowhere at all. They are not the same question:
# zealous is how loudly a man believes, and piety level is what his faith
# thinks of HIM - a septon-approved lord of great standing can be quite cold
# about it, and a fanatic who married his sister has nothing in that account.
# In a setting where a war can be preached, standing is the number that says
# whether the men think the gods are on this side of the field.
#
# LOSING, AND HE DOES NOT MOVE. That is the whole template. The day is going
# against a man whose faith has always told him it would not, and what he does
# with that is plant himself where the banner is and let the line form on him.
# The prose must not decide whether it works.
#
# `entrenched`/`up` AND NO RUNG. bc_shieldwall is what the seam hands out for
# entrenched: men who stop giving ground. bc_they_fear_me_more reaches the same
# cell on a losing field from dread and takes a guarded momentum rung with it;
# this one deliberately does not, because a host that has decided the gods are
# watching is not winning anything back yet - it is refusing to move, which is
# exactly what the modifier says and no more.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_gods_are_watching = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		piety_level >= 3
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			has_trait = zealous
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_foe_is_heathen_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = cynical
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_gods_are_watching"

		add_piety = 50

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_gods_are_watching_toast
			desc = bc_the_gods_are_watching_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE BODY, FROM THE SADDLE, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE HEALTH TRAITS APPEAR ZERO TIMES IN THIS FILE. The knight side got them
# in the body batch - `wounded_*`, `maimed`/`one_legged`, `one_eyed`/
# `disfigured`, three templates - on the argument that prowess is a number
# about how well a man fights and not about what he is fighting with. That
# argument is not weaker for a commander; it is different. A knight's body
# decides what he can do in the next four feet, and a commander's decides
# whether he can be at the other end of the field in the next quarter hour.
#
# THESE TWO ARE ABOUT REACH, NOT ABOUT COURAGE OR PAIN. The knight templates
# are written from inside the ranks and can afford to be about how it feels.
# From the saddle the only thing a wound changes that an army notices is where
# the man can get to and how fast, so that is what both descs are about.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He knows where he is needed and it is a long way off.
#
# THE SAME `wounded_*` LADDER AS bc_knight_the_old_wound AND THE SAME LOSING
# ROW, in the other event pool. The two never compete - a `type = commander`
# template and a `type = knight` template are rolled from different lists - so
# this is not the distinctness question at all; it is the same fact asked of
# the man whose job is the whole field instead of his own part of it.
#
# `measured`/`down`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING. He is not doing anything wrong
# and the army is not coming apart: the orders are correct and they are
# arriving late, because the man who has to see the thing before he can order
# it cannot get to where it can be seen. There is no army modifier for that
# and there should not be - the faltering rung is the whole cost.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_he_cannot_get_there = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		OR = {
			has_trait = wounded_1
			has_trait = wounded_2
			has_trait = wounded_3
		}
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			has_trait = wounded_3
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_host_is_vast_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = strategist
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_he_cannot_get_there"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_he_cannot_get_there_toast
			desc = bc_he_cannot_get_there_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man who stopped trying to be everywhere years ago.
#
# NO SIDE ROW, AND THAT IS THE DIFFERENCE FROM THE TEMPLATE ABOVE. A wound is
# a thing that happened to him and is still deciding his afternoon; a leg he
# has not had for ten years is a thing he has already built a method around.
# The first is about a gap and this is about what fills it - he picks one
# place, he makes everybody come to him, and the host learns the shape of that
# in the first hour.
#
# `adapted`/`up` AND A GUARDED MOMENTUM RUNG. `adapted` is the seam's word for
# how the commander READ the battle rather than what the battle did to the
# army, and it hands out no modifier by design - which is exactly right here,
# because nothing about the men changed. What changed is that the man running
# them has a working answer to a problem he did not choose, and the rung is
# where that shows.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_he_holds_one_place = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		OR = {
			has_trait = maimed
			has_trait = one_legged
			# THE SAME AGOT GAP AS bc_knight_will_not_be_carried'S, AND FIXED WITH IT.
			# `one_handed` is AGOT's own maiming trait, 00_agot_traits.txt:1411.
			has_trait = one_handed
		}
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = strategist
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_he_holds_one_place"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_he_holds_one_place_toast
			desc = bc_he_holds_one_place_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE OTHER THREE NUMBERS, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE MOD HAS READ EXACTLY TWO OF THE FIVE SKILLS. `martial` appears twelve
# times in this file and `prowess` fourteen times in the knight file, and
# `diplomacy`, `intrigue` and `learning` appear ZERO times anywhere in the
# mod - not in a gate, not in a `chance` row, not in a comment. That is a
# defensible default for a battle mod and it is also the reason every
# commander case reads like the same man: forty-three templates and the only
# thing any of them knows how to say about competence is how good he is at
# fighting.
#
# THE THREE ARE NOT A LADDER AND MUST NOT BE WRITTEN AS ONE. They are three
# different claims about why a man is standing where he is standing, and each
# one takes the side row that makes its own claim legible:
#
#   intrigue  - he arranged something the other man did not see. Winning,
#               because a trick that failed is not a trick anybody writes down.
#   learning  - he knows the shape of what is happening because he has read
#               about it happening before. Losing, because knowing the shape
#               of a rout is only worth anything while there is still time.
#   diplomacy - the lords under him came for HIM. Losing, because a host that
#               is winning does not have to be held together.
#
# THE BAR IS 14 ON ALL THREE, which is the same bar this file already uses
# for `martial` in four places. It is deliberately not 16: these are not
# gates on a legendary schemer, they are gates on a man who is genuinely good
# at the thing, and 16 in a non-martial skill is rare enough in an AGOT save
# that the templates would hardly ever be read.
#
# NONE OF THE THREE COLLIDES WITH THE EDUCATION BATCH ABOVE, and two of them
# can be true of the same man at the same time as bc_never_learned_the_trade
# - a Citadel-taught heir losing a battle satisfies that gate AND the
# learning gate here. That is correct and not a bug: they are separate rolls
# in one pool, only one fires, and they are not the same sentence. One says
# he is not a soldier. The other says he can see exactly what is about to
# happen. A maester's son on a bad afternoon is both.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man who won this an hour ago, somewhere the other commander could not
# see.
#
# WINNING IS PART OF THE CLAIM, not a mood row. A feint is only a feint in
# retrospect; on a losing field the identical order is a mistake, and the mod
# already owns that sentence three or four times over.
#
# `adapted`/`up`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING. bc_pursuit would be the obvious
# reward and it is wrong here - pursuit is what a host gets for doing the
# correct thing at the correct moment, and this host did not do anything
# except arrive where it was sent. The whole gain is the momentum rung,
# because what changed is the situation and not the men.
#
# NO `martial` ROW ANYWHERE IN THE CHANCE BLOCK, on purpose. Weighting this
# by martial would quietly turn it back into a template about a good soldier,
# which is the thing the batch exists to stop.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_trick_they_did_not_see = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		intrigue >= 14
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			intrigue >= 18
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = deceitful
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_trick_they_did_not_see"

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_trick_they_did_not_see_toast
			desc = bc_the_trick_they_did_not_see_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man who recognises this. Not because he has done it, because he has
# read it.
#
# LOSING AND `measured`/`up`, WHICH IS THE UNUSUAL PART. Almost every losing
# template in this file takes `down`. The claim is narrow and specific: he is
# not turning the battle around, he is seeing the collapse one step before it
# arrives and giving the order that stops it being a collapse. The rung is
# the whole of it. `measured` hands out no modifier, which is right - there
# is no army modifier for a commander recognising a shape.
#
# IT IS NOT A SECOND bc_the_gods_are_watching. That one is a man refusing to
# move and taking no rung at all; this one is a man moving early and taking
# the rung. The two share a side row and nothing else.
#
# `NOT = { has_trait = education_martial_4 }` IS DELIBERATELY ABSENT. A
# martially-raised man with learning 14 is a perfectly good subject for this
# and excluding him would be the template arguing that reading and training
# are alternatives. They are not.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_he_has_read_about_this = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		learning >= 14
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			learning >= 18
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = education_learning_4
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_he_has_read_about_this"

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_he_has_read_about_this_toast
			desc = bc_he_has_read_about_this_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The host that gives ground in one piece because the men holding the flanks
# are the men who chose to come.
#
# THIS IS NOT THE DREAD AXIS AGAIN AND THE DISTINCTION IS THE WHOLE POINT.
# bc_they_fear_me_more is a host that holds because leaving is worse than
# staying. This is a host that holds because the lords in it like the man
# they came with. The two produce the same shieldwall and they are not the
# same fact about him, and a commander can score high on both - the pool
# rolls one.
#
# `entrenched`/`down` IS THE RAREST CELL IN THE SEAM and it is the correct
# one. `entrenched` hands out bc_shieldwall; `down` takes the faltering rung.
# Together they say exactly what is happening: the host is losing, and it is
# losing in formation rather than in pieces. Every other losing template that
# grants a modifier is granting it for something the men did; this one grants
# it for something the man is.
#
# NO STRESS. He is not paying for this - the whole content of the moment is
# that the thing holding is not costing him anything, because it was built
# years ago at somebody else's table.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_they_came_for_him = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		diplomacy >= 14
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			diplomacy >= 18
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_they_came_for_him"

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = bc_they_came_for_him_toast
			desc = bc_they_came_for_him_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE MAN ON THE OTHER SIDE IS ALSO COMMANDING, 2026-08-17.
#
# FORTY-SIX COMMANDER TEMPLATES AND EVERY SINGLE ONE OF THEM IS ABOUT THIS
# SIDE OF THE FIELD. The three foe gates the mod shipped read the enemy
# commander's blood, his house, his faith and his tongue - who he is - and
# not one thing about what he is doing. The odds triggers read the other host
# as a number. The result is that every chronicle in the mod is written as
# though a commander were playing against the ground and the arithmetic, and
# the other man were a fact about the terrain.
#
# THE TWO GATES ARE bc_foe_is_formidable_trigger AND bc_foe_is_outmatched_
# trigger, both new in bc_scripted_triggers.txt and both reading `martial` on
# scope:combat_side.enemy_side.side_commander. The reasoning for martial over
# prowess, and for the 16/8 bars, is recorded there and is not repeated here.
#
# NEITHER TEMPLATE MAY BE WRITTEN AS A JUDGEMENT ON THE OTHER MAN'S
# CHARACTER. The mod does not know whether he is brave or a coward and has no
# business guessing; it knows one number about his ability to run a field.
# Every sentence in the loc for these has to be about what is arriving over
# here, because that is genuinely all our commander can see.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Nothing he tries is landing, and it is not the ground and it is not the
# numbers.
#
# LOSING IS PART OF THE CLAIM. On a winning field the same fact is just a
# compliment paid to a man who is about to be beaten anyway, and there is no
# moment in it.
#
# `adapted`/`down`, THE SECOND-RAREST CELL IN THE SEAM, AND THE ONLY ONE THAT
# SAYS THIS. `adapted` is the KIND for a commander reading the battle, and it
# hands out no modifier by design. Paired with `down` it says the thing this
# template exists to say: he is reading it, he is reading it correctly, he has
# changed the plan twice, and each change has been answered before it
# finished. bc_never_learned_the_trade is a man out of his depth; this is a
# man at exactly his depth against somebody deeper, which is a different
# afternoon.
#
# THE STRESS IS THE COST AND THERE IS NO OTHER. There is no army modifier for
# being outthought - the men are doing what they were told - so the faltering
# rung and ten stress are the whole of it.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_other_man_is_better = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_foe_is_formidable_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = strategist
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_other_man_is_better"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_other_man_is_better_toast
			desc = bc_the_other_man_is_better_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The other side has just done something nobody who had been taught would do.
#
# NO SIDE ROW AT ALL, AND THAT IS THE INTERESTING HALF. A mistake across the
# field is worth exactly the same whether we are ahead or behind - arguably
# more when we are behind, because it is the only thing on offer - and gating
# this on winning would turn it into a victory lap. It is one of the few
# templates in the file that can fire on a field that is going badly and still
# hand out a momentum rung, which is precisely the shape of the moment.
#
# `ordered`/`up`, WHICH IS bc_pursuit. This is the one place in the batch a
# modifier is correct: something has opened over there and the answer is men
# going through it at speed. The seam already hands bc_pursuit out for doing
# the right thing at the right moment; here the right moment was made by
# somebody else.
#
# IT IS NOT A TEMPLATE ABOUT OUR COMMANDER BEING CLEVER. The `martial` chance
# rows below weight it, because a better soldier notices sooner, but the gate
# is only about the other man. A commander of 6 whose enemy is a 4 gets this
# too, and should.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_gap_he_left = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_foe_is_outmatched_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = strategist
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_gap_he_left"

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_gap_he_left_toast
			desc = bc_the_gap_he_left_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE NAME HE ALREADY HAS, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE MOD HANDS OUT NICKNAMES AND HAS NEVER ONCE READ ONE BACK. bc_reward_
# effects.txt carries three pools and thirty-five keys of them; the aftermath
# awards one at bc_reputation_effects.txt phase 2c; and the only place in the
# entire mod that asks whether a character has a nickname is the guard on that
# award, `has_any_nickname = no`, which exists purely to avoid overwriting one.
# The mod is therefore a machine that produces reputation and cannot see it.
#
# THAT IS THE SAME SHAPE AS THE STRESS BATCH AND THE RELATION BATCH, and it is
# the most productive question this session has found: what does this mod
# write that it never reads? A nickname is the loudest of them, because unlike
# stress or a relation it is a thing OTHER PEOPLE say out loud, which is
# exactly what a battlefield is full of.
#
# `has_any_nickname`, NOT `has_nickname = nick_x`. The gate must not care WHICH
# name. A test against a specific key would tie the templates to AGOT's
# nickname list, and half the men who walk onto a field in Westeros with a name
# on them got it somewhere this mod has never heard of.
#
# IT IS NOT THE DREAD AXIS AND IT IS NOT THE PIETY AXIS. Dread is what his own
# men are afraid of. A nickname is what everybody who has never met him
# expects, which is a fact about the world and not about the host - and both
# templates below are written from that difference.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The name is doing some of the work.
#
# `wild`/`up`, WHICH IS bc_spent_horse AND A MOMENTUM RUNG IN THE SAME BREATH,
# and it is the correct pair rather than a compromise. A host going forward
# behind a name it is shouting is not a host being handled well; it is a host
# that has stopped waiting to be told. It gains the rung because it is
# genuinely winning ground, and it blows the horses because nobody in it is
# pacing anything. bc_the_wrath_takes_him and bc_the_old_grudge reach the same
# cell out of the commander's temper; this reaches it out of his reputation,
# which he may not even want.
#
# WINNING IS PART OF THE CLAIM. The name only carries a host that is already
# going forward - the losing half of the axis is the template below, and it is
# a completely different sentence.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_they_are_shouting_his_name = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_any_nickname = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			prestige_level >= 3
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_they_are_shouting_his_name"

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_they_are_shouting_his_name_toast
			desc = bc_they_are_shouting_his_name_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The other end of the same fact. Everyone on this field turned up expecting
# the man in the song, including the men standing behind him.
#
# `measured`/`down`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING, AND THAT IS THE POINT. There is
# no army modifier for a reputation failing to hold, because nothing about the
# army has changed - the men are where they were put and they are doing what
# they were told. What has changed is what they thought this was going to be.
#
# FIFTEEN STRESS, WHICH IS MORE THAN THE FILE'S USUAL TEN, and the extra five
# is the whole argument of the template. Every other losing case costs him ten
# because losing costs ten. This one costs more because he is losing in front
# of a name, and the name will still be attached to him next year.
#
# NO `prestige_level` FLOOR ON THE GATE. A minor lord who got a name once for
# something small is exactly as exposed as a famous one, arguably more, and a
# floor here would quietly make the template about greatness instead of about
# expectation.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_name_is_a_weight = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_any_nickname = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			prestige_level >= 3
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_name_is_a_weight"

		add_stress = 15

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_name_is_a_weight_toast
			desc = bc_the_name_is_a_weight_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE MOD READS ITS OWN ARMY STATE BACK, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE FOUR VERB MODIFIERS ARE WRITTEN A HUNDRED AND FOUR TIMES AND READ ZERO
# TIMES. bc_bloodied, bc_shieldwall, bc_spent_horse and bc_pursuit are handed
# out by bc_apply_verb_effect at every call site in the mod and taken away
# again by bc_cleanup_effect at combat end, and between those two moments
# absolutely nothing in the mod ever asks whether one of them is live. The
# rungs are read - bc_has_momentum_trigger and bc_is_faltering_trigger exist
# for exactly that - and the verbs are not.
#
# READING THEM MAKES MOMENTS COMPOUND, WHICH THE MOD HAS NEVER DONE. Every
# template shipped so far is written as though its battle started five minutes
# ago: it asks about the man, the ground, the odds and the foe, and never once
# about what has already happened on this field. A gate on a live verb
# modifier is the first thing in the mod that can say "again".
#
# WHAT A LIVE VERB MODIFIER ACTUALLY MEANS, PRECISELY. bc_add_verb_effect
# grants 20 days and bc_cleanup_effect wipes all four at combat end, so during
# a battle a live verb was granted either by an earlier moment in THIS battle
# or by the eve event immediately before it. It is never a leftover from last
# month's war. The descs may therefore say "already today" and may not say
# "in the last battle", and that distinction is load-bearing rather than
# stylistic.
#
# ROOT IS THE COMMANDER IN THIS FILE AND THE MODIFIERS LIVE ON THE COMMANDER,
# so a bare `has_character_modifier` is correct here and ONLY here. The knight
# file's copy of this batch has to reach through scope:combat_side.side_
# commander for the same reason its momentum guards do, which is recorded in
# that file's tenth batch header.
#
# NEITHER TEMPLATE RE-GRANTS THE MODIFIER THAT GATED IT. That was available -
# a deepening shieldwall is a real thing - and it is deliberately not done,
# because a template whose grant satisfies its own gate will read as the same
# toast twice to anybody unlucky with the cooldown.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The field is theirs and there is nothing left to take it with.
#
# `ordered`/`down` ON A WINNING FIELD, AND SWING IS NOT THE BATTLE. SWING is
# the direction of the MOMENT, and this moment goes the wrong way in the
# middle of an afternoon that is going the right way. The seam has always
# allowed this and bc_he_will_not_chase_them is the shipped precedent.
#
# IT IS THE SECOND TEMPLATE ABOUT A PURSUIT THAT DOES NOT HAPPEN AND THAT IS
# INTENDED. bc_he_will_not_chase_them is a decision - he could and he will
# not, because the men running away have his name. This is arithmetic: he
# would, and the horses did it an hour ago. The two are as different as a debt
# and a gift and the loc is written to keep them apart.
#
# NO RUNG AT ALL. A momentum rung would be absurd on a moment that is about
# not converting one, and a faltering rung would tell the player the battle is
# slipping when it is not. The stress is the only cost, and it is the right
# one: this is a man watching something he cannot buy walk away.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_horses_are_already_gone = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_character_modifier = bc_spent_horse
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_horses_are_already_gone"

		add_stress = 10

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = bc_the_horses_are_already_gone_toast
			desc = bc_the_horses_are_already_gone_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# An hour ago these men were the ones doing the chasing.
#
# THE GATE IS A LIVE bc_pursuit ON A LOSING FIELD, which is a state the mod
# has been producing since plan 1 and has never been able to notice: something
# earlier today went well enough to hand this host a pursuit, and the
# afternoon has turned over on top of it. Nothing else in the mod can say that
# sentence, because nothing else in the mod remembers.
#
# `bloodied`/`down`: bc_bloodied AND the faltering rung. The turn is not free.
# Men who were running forward in loose order half an hour ago are the worst
# possible men to be receiving a push, and the cost lands on the army rather
# than on the commander's judgement - which is why this is bloodied and not
# measured.
#
# IT DOES NOT REMOVE bc_pursuit. Tempting, and wrong: the verb families are
# owned by bc_cleanup_effect and a content template reaching into them would
# put modifier lifetime in two places. The pursuit expiring on its own 20-day
# timer or at combat end is correct, and the toast simply describes a host
# that still has the modifier and no longer deserves it.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_they_were_chasing_an_hour_ago = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_character_modifier = bc_pursuit
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial <= 8
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_they_were_chasing_an_hour_ago"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_they_were_chasing_an_hour_ago_toast
			desc = bc_they_were_chasing_an_hour_ago_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE LEDGER AS A GATE, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE MOD HAS COUNTED ITS OWN MOMENTS SINCE PLAN 1 AND HAS NEVER ASKED THE
# COUNT A QUESTION. `var:bc_battle_moments` is incremented by
# bc_record_moment_effect at every call site, and it is read in exactly three
# places: the tier ladder inside the ledger, the first-moment `exists` test
# that opens a battle, and the eve's guard. Not one template anywhere in the
# mod gates on how much has already happened on this field.
#
# THIS IS THE SECOND HALF OF THE VERB-MODIFIER BATCH AND A BIGGER ONE. A live
# verb says something specific happened; the ledger count says the afternoon
# has length. Together they are the whole of what the mod remembers about a
# battle in progress, and until today it remembered both and used neither.
#
# THE THRESHOLD IS 3, AND IT IS NOT ARBITRARY. The ledger's own tier ladder
# treats 3 as the major rung and 4 as decisive, so 3 is the first count the
# mod already considers meaningful. The measured rate is roughly 4.2 moments
# per battle, which means this gate opens on a clear majority of battles but
# only in their second half - which is exactly the shape wanted.
#
# NO SIDE ROW. Length is not a side. A battle that has produced three moments
# is long whether it is being won or lost, and adding a side row would halve
# the reach of a gate whose entire content is duration.
#
# THE COUNT LIVES ON THE COMMANDER, so a bare `var:bc_battle_moments` is
# correct in this file and would be silently always-false in the knight file -
# bc_scripted_triggers.txt records that trap in full on
# bc_chronicle_ready_trigger, and the knight template in this batch reaches
# through scope:combat_side.side_commander for that reason.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# It is not going to be over quickly, and everybody on the field has worked
# that out at about the same time.
#
# `entrenched`/`up`: bc_shieldwall AND a momentum rung. A fight that has gone
# on this long is a fight where both lines have stopped trying to be clever;
# the shieldwall is literal and the rung is the fact that his host is still a
# host after three of these. It is not a claim that he is winning - there is
# no side row - it is a claim that what is left of the afternoon will be
# decided by whoever is still standing in order.
#
# IT CANNOT RUN AWAY WITH ITSELF. The gate gets easier as a battle goes on,
# which would be a problem if the pool were unlimited; it is not.
# bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger fails while bc_chronicle_recent is
# live, so this competes for the same rate-limited slot as everything else and
# a long battle simply makes it likelier to own one of them - which is the
# intent.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_this_has_been_a_long_afternoon = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		var:bc_battle_moments >= 3
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			var:bc_battle_moments >= 5
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_this_has_been_a_long_afternoon"

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = bc_this_has_been_a_long_afternoon_toast
			desc = bc_this_has_been_a_long_afternoon_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE TOP OF THE LADDER, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE RUNG MODIFIERS ARE READ IN EXACTLY ONE WAY AND IT IS NOT A QUESTION.
# bc_has_momentum_trigger and bc_is_faltering_trigger are `OR` arms over the
# three tiers, and every single use of them in the mod is a `NOT` inside a
# grant - a guard that stops a second rung landing on a commander who already
# has one. The mod therefore knows the difference between minor, major and
# decisive momentum, writes it on the commander, and has never once asked
# WHICH rung a host is on.
#
# THE DECISIVE RUNG IS THE RAREST STATE THE MOD PRODUCES. It takes a chain of
# moments to reach, it is the loudest thing on the commander's modifier list,
# and nothing has ever been written about being there. That is the gap this
# batch fills, and it is deliberately only the top tier: a template gated on
# minor momentum would fire in most battles the mod touches and would say
# nothing, because minor momentum is the mod's ordinary weather.
#
# NEITHER TEMPLATE GRANTS A RUNG, AND NOT BECAUSE OF THE GUARD. The guard
# would block it anyway - that is what the guard is for - but writing the
# `if` and letting it fail silently would be a lie in the source. A host at
# the top of the ladder has nowhere to be moved to, and the template says so
# by not trying.
#
# THE COMMANDER'S GATE IS A BARE has_character_modifier AND THE KNIGHT'S IS
# NOT. Same reason as the verb batch above and the ledger batch above that:
# the rung families live on the side commander, and this file is rooted on
# him.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The host is at the top of the ladder and everybody in it can feel the
# floor.
#
# NO SIDE ROW, BECAUSE THE RUNG IS THE SIDE ROW. A commander does not carry
# bc_momentum_decisive on a field that is going badly - it takes a chain of
# won moments to get there - so asking bc_side_is_winning_trigger as well
# would be asking the same question twice and narrowing the gate for nothing.
#
# `ordered`/`up`: bc_pursuit, and it is the one modifier that means anything
# to a host in this state. It has already been given every other advantage
# the seam hands out at some point on the way up the ladder; what it does not
# have is permission to keep going, and that is exactly what this hands it.
#
# NO STRESS EITHER WAY. Nothing is being paid for here. The template exists to
# name a state the mod produces and has never described, and a resource line
# would turn it into a transaction.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_nothing_is_stopping_this = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_character_modifier = bc_momentum_decisive
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_nothing_is_stopping_this"

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_nothing_is_stopping_this_toast
			desc = bc_nothing_is_stopping_this_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# WHAT THE HOST HAD OPINIONS ABOUT THIS MORNING, 2026-08-17.
#
# TWO FACTS ABOUT A COMMANDER THAT WESTEROS ARGUES ABOUT BEFORE IT ARGUES
# ABOUT TACTICS, AND THE MOD READS NEITHER. `is_female` appears ZERO times
# anywhere in the mod. `is_lowborn` appears three times in the knight file and
# once in this one, in a comment. Fifty-three commander templates ask what he
# is good at, what he was raised to, what he owns, what he believes, what he
# is afraid of and what shape his body is in, and not one of them asks the two
# questions a Westerosi host asks about a new commander before it has seen him
# do anything.
#
# BOTH TAKE THE WINNING ROW AND THAT IS NOT A COINCIDENCE. On a losing field
# either fact turns into the host's explanation for the defeat, which is a
# sentence about prejudice being confirmed, and this mod does not write it -
# not because it is uncomfortable but because it is a claim about what men
# think that the code cannot support. Winning, the same fact is a thing that
# has visibly stopped mattering in the last hour, which the code CAN support:
# the side row is the evidence.
#
# NEITHER IS THE STATION AXIS AGAIN. bc_nothing_but_the_horse gates on
# `is_landed = no` and is about what a man owns. Birth is not tenure - a
# hedge knight of a great house is landless and not lowborn, and the two
# templates are about different arguments. This is the same distinction
# bc_knight_no_house_behind_him records on the knight side.
#
# THE FEMALE CASE IS NOT RARE IN AGOT AND THE TEMPLATE IS NOT A NOVELTY. The
# North, the Iron Islands and Dorne all put women in command in the source
# material, and CK3's own succession laws produce them everywhere else. The
# desc is written about a host that has stopped discussing it, not about the
# discussion.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The orders are simply being taken now.
#
# `ordered`/`up`: bc_pursuit and a guarded momentum rung. This is the cell for
# a host doing the correct thing at the correct moment, and that is exactly
# the content - not that she is clever, but that the gap between an order
# leaving her and a line moving has closed to nothing over the course of one
# morning.
#
# THE `chance` ROWS ARE ABOUT COMPETENCE AND NOTHING ELSE. martial and
# strategist, the same two rows half this file carries. Weighting this by a
# temperament trait would be the template having a theory about what kind of
# woman commands well, and it does not have one.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_orders_are_taken = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		is_female = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = strategist
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_orders_are_taken"

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_orders_are_taken_toast
			desc = bc_the_orders_are_taken_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man with no name at all is winning somebody else's battle for them.
#
# SEVENTY-FIVE PRESTIGE, WHICH IS ABOVE THIS FILE'S USUAL FIFTY, and the
# reason is the same one bc_knight_no_house_behind_him gives for its 150:
# prestige is the only currency a man without a name can be paid in. It is
# not the knight's number, because a commander who is lowborn has already
# been given something a hedge knight never gets - the command - and the
# award should not pretend otherwise.
#
# `adapted`/`up`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING. There is no army modifier for this.
# The men are doing what they were doing an hour ago; what has changed is who
# they will say did it. The rung is the whole mechanical content and the
# prestige is the whole personal content, and that split is the template.
#
# NO STRESS ROW EITHER DIRECTION. Relief would make it a template about him
# being validated, which is smaller than what is happening.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_no_name_at_all = {

			factor = 0.5

		}

			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander

# ===========================================================================
# WHOSE WAR IT IS, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE MOD HAS ASKED THIS QUESTION ONCE, IN ONE DIRECTION, SINCE PLAN 1.
# `side_primary_participant` appears in exactly one gate anywhere -
# bc_his_lords_war, which asks whether the commander is NOT the war leader -
# and nowhere else. The affirmative case and the entire other side of the
# field were never asked. Three templates here: the man whose war it is
# losing it, the man who started it standing across the field, and a knight
# looking at the same banner from four feet lower down.
#
# THE AFFIRMATIVE TRIGGER IS NOT THE NEGATION OF THE SHIPPED ONE, and the
# header on bc_this_is_his_own_war_trigger says why at length: a side with no
# war leader answers no to the shipped trigger because of its `exists`, so
# `NOT = { bc_fighting_his_lords_war_trigger = yes }` would answer YES there.
# Both had to be written affirmatively.
#
# bc_foe_leader_commands_in_person_trigger IS THE FIRST TRIGGER IN THE MOD
# THAT COMPARES TWO MEMBERS OF ONE SIDE. Everything else reads a property off
# a character. This one asks whether the enemy's commander and the enemy's
# war leader are the same man, which is the only thing available here that
# separates a lord's own host from a host his castellan is walking about.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_facing_nemesis_trigger AGAIN. That one is about a personal
# relation between two men; this is about an office. A man can be commanding
# in person and be nothing to you, and your nemesis can be somebody's
# under-captain. The two are orthogonal and neither implies the other.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The war has his name on it and it is going badly.
#
# THE ONE THING THIS TEMPLATE HAS THAT NO OTHER LOSING TEMPLATE HAS is that
# there is nobody above him in it. Every other man on this field can tell
# himself afterwards that he was sent. The desc is about that and not about
# guilt, which is a bigger word than the situation needs.
#
# `measured`/`down`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING. There is no army modifier for a
# man understanding his own position, and the faltering rung is the whole
# mechanical cost. Ten stress, the file's usual.
#
# NO PRESTIGE EITHER DIRECTION. Losing a war of your own is expensive in ways
# the aftermath already prices; charging for it twice at the phase level
# would double-count.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_war_has_his_name_on_it = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_this_is_his_own_war_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who thinks well of himself has further to fall in one morning.
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_war_has_his_name_on_it"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_war_has_his_name_on_it_toast
			desc = bc_the_war_has_his_name_on_it_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man who started it is standing over there.
#
# THE WINNING ROW IS LOAD-BEARING AND IT IS NOT DECORATION. The content is
# that the one man who could end this war by changing his mind is on the
# field and having a bad afternoon, which is only true on the winning side.
# Losing, the same gate is a template about being frightened of a banner, and
# the mod has three of those already.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE, THE FILE'S USUAL. It is not the hedge knight's number and
# not the lowborn's: nothing has been proved about who he is, only about who
# is in front of him.
#
# `bloodied`/`up`. A host that has worked out whose banner that is fights the
# next quarter hour harder and worse, and bc_bloodied is exactly that trade.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_man_who_started_it = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_foe_leader_commands_in_person_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 14
		}
		# A man who keeps accounts notices whose name is at the top of this one.
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = vengeful
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_man_who_started_it"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_man_who_started_it_toast
			desc = bc_the_man_who_started_it_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# WHY HE IS HERE, 2026-08-17.
#
# `has_hook` APPEARED NOWHERE IN THE MOD. Fifty-eight commander templates ask
# what a man can do, what he owns, what he believes, what he fears and whose
# war it is, and not one asks the only question the game can actually answer
# about WHY he came: whether somebody is holding something over him. The mod
# has been writing about obligation since plan 1 - bc_his_lords_war,
# bc_nothing_but_the_horse, the whole station batch - on inference.
#
# THIS IS THE STATION AXIS' THIRD VISIT AND IT IS NOT THE SAME QUESTION.
# bc_nothing_but_the_horse asks what he owns, bc_the_war_has_his_name_on_it
# asks whose quarrel it is, and this asks what it cost him to be asked. A
# landed man with a name on the war can still be here on a leash.
#
# THE TWO TEMPLATES ARE THE SAME TWO CHARACTERS POINTED OPPOSITE WAYS and
# they take opposite side rows for it. Held, on a field going badly: the worst
# afternoon in the mod to be a man who did not choose this. Holding, on a
# field going well: a debt being paid in the only currency that clears it.
#
# BOTH TRIGGERS CAN BE TRUE AT ONCE - two men can hold hooks on each other -
# and the side rows keep the two templates out of the same battle in the
# ordinary case without the mod having to describe the knot.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He was made to come and it is going badly.
#
# `measured`/`down`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING, and that is the correct
# mechanical content twice over: there is no army modifier for a man
# understanding what he is doing here, and the men do not know any of this.
# The faltering rung and ten stress are the whole cost.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT LET HIM CONSIDER LEAVING. He is a commander in the middle
# of a battle; the leash is why he is on the field and not a live option he is
# weighing this hour. A template that makes it a decision has written a
# different and much smaller moment.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_thing_they_have_on_him = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_his_lord_holds_a_hook_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 14
		}
		# A man who expects to be used notices sooner that he is being used.
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = cynical
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_thing_they_have_on_him"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_thing_they_have_on_him_toast
			desc = bc_the_thing_they_have_on_him_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He came holding the leash, and he is winning with it.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE AND `adapted`/`up`. He is not doing anything on the field
# that the men can see is different; what has changed is the size of the
# account, and prestige is the only part of that the mod can actually settle.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE HOOK. The mod does not know what it is - a secret,
# a debt, a favour, a letter - and a desc that invents one is writing content
# the save file will contradict. What it may say is that the man at the top of
# this war owes him one more thing than he did at dawn.
#
# NO STRESS ROW EITHER DIRECTION. Relief would make it a template about being
# safe, which is not what is happening.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_leash_runs_the_other_way = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_he_holds_his_lords_hook_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			intrigue >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_leash_runs_the_other_way"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_leash_runs_the_other_way_toast
			desc = bc_the_leash_runs_the_other_way_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE OFFICE HE HOLDS AT HOME, 2026-08-17.
#
# `has_council_position` APPEARED NOWHERE IN THE MOD. Sixty commander
# templates and nothing has ever asked what job a man does when there is no
# battle - which is a strange gap in a game where the council seat is very
# often the reason he is the one holding the host at all. CK3 sends the
# marshal; AGOT sends the castellan when the marshal is dead.
#
# TWO SEATS, NOT EIGHT, AND THE OTHER SIX ARE NOT OVERSIGHTS. A steward, a
# spymaster or a chaplain in command is a man doing something other than his
# job, and the mod already has a template for a man out of his depth. The
# marshal and the castellan are the two seats that make a claim about how he
# ought to command, and the claims are opposite: one has drilled these men for
# years and one has spent those years behind a wall.
#
# NO TRIGGER FOR EITHER. `has_council_position` reads off the character in
# scope and answers no for a man who holds no seat, so both gates are a single
# inline row and neither needs an `exists`. Only the Kingsguard needed a
# scripted trigger, because AGOT models the order as seven separate positions.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The marshal is watching the thing he built do what he built it for.
#
# `ordered`/`up` AND bc_pursuit, WHICH IS THE ONE CELL THAT FITS. The claim is
# not that he is clever today; it is that the host is executing, and the
# reason it is executing is two years of winters he spent on it. That is
# exactly what the `ordered` row's `up` half is for.
#
# NOT bc_the_orders_are_taken WITH A DIFFERENT GATE. That template is about a
# gap closing over the course of one morning. This one is about a thing that
# was already true before anybody rode out, and the desc has to be written at
# that distance - the drill, not the afternoon.
#
# NO PRESTIGE. Doing your own job well is not a deed.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_marshals_own_work = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_council_position = councillor_marshal
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 16
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = diligent
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_marshals_own_work"

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_marshals_own_work_toast
			desc = bc_the_marshals_own_work_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The castellan is outdoors.
#
# `entrenched`/`down` AND bc_shieldwall, AND THE MODIFIER IS THE JOKE. A man
# who has spent his life minding a wall reaches for the thing that has always
# worked, which is to close up and let them come, and out here that is how a
# host is surrounded rather than how it is saved. The mod hands him the
# shieldwall because that is what he did; whether it was right is the
# aftermath's business.
#
# THIS IS NOT A TEMPLATE ABOUT A COWARD OR A FOOL. He is good at a job that is
# not this one, and the desc has to keep that respect or it turns into
# bc_knight_shames_himself with a council seat.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_castellan_outdoors = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_council_position = councillor_castellan
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			stewardship >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_castellan_outdoors"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_castellan_outdoors_toast
			desc = bc_the_castellan_outdoors_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A black brother is commanding, and there are fewer of them than there were.
#
# THE FIRST AGOT-SPECIFIC CHARACTER GATE IN THE MOD. Everything else here is
# vanilla vocabulary that AGOT happens to inherit - skills, traits, council
# seats, terrain helpers. `agot_is_member_of_nights_watch` is AGOT's own
# trigger, defined in common/scripted_triggers/00_agot_nights_watch_triggers.txt
# as an OR over `nightswatch`, `nightswatch_temp` and `nightswatch_historical`.
#
# CALL AGOT'S TRIGGER, DO NOT INLINE THE THREE TRAITS. The mod already takes
# this dependency for the terrain helpers and for the same reason: if AGOT
# adds a fourth way to be in the Watch, an inlined OR goes quietly wrong and
# a called trigger does not.
#
# THE LOSING ROW IS THE ONLY ROW THIS TEMPLATE COULD TAKE. The Watch's
# arithmetic is that it is always fewer than it was and nobody is sending
# more, which is a sentence about a bad afternoon and reads as self-pity on a
# good one.
#
# `bloodied`/`down` AND bc_bloodied. The men fight harder and worse because
# there is nowhere behind them to be sent to, which is exactly the trade that
# modifier makes.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_watch_has_no_reserve = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		agot_is_member_of_nights_watch = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = humble
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_watch_has_no_reserve"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_watch_has_no_reserve_toast
			desc = bc_the_watch_has_no_reserve_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE TWO CELLS THE EMPTY PURSE NEVER FILLED, 2026-08-17.
#
# `gold < 0` IS ALREADY IN THE MOD TWICE AND BOTH TIMES ON THE SAME DIAGONAL.
# bc_the_chest_is_empty is a commander who is broke and losing;
# bc_knight_the_ransom_he_needs is a knight who is broke and winning. The
# other diagonal - broke and winning on the commander side, broke and losing
# on the knight side - was never written, and it is not the same moment with
# the sign flipped. Money does something different to a man who is ahead than
# to a man who is behind, and it does something different again to a man whose
# own body is the collateral.
#
# NOT A SUBSET OF EITHER SHIPPED TEMPLATE. Same character gate, opposite side
# row, and the side rows are mutually exclusive, so neither pair can compete.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He is winning and he cannot afford to be here tomorrow.
#
# THE WINNING HALF OF bc_the_chest_is_empty AND NOT ITS MIRROR. That template
# is about men who have started weighing their loyalty; this one is about a
# commander who has done the sum himself and found that the only affordable
# battle is a short one. The men in this one are not owed an explanation and
# are not asking for one.
#
# `wild`/`up` AND bc_spent_horse, WHERE THE LOSING HALF TOOK `wild`/`down`.
# Same KIND on purpose: an empty chest makes a man reckless in both directions
# and the mod should say so with the same word. Upward it means he spends the
# horses to end it this afternoon, which is exactly what the modifier costs.
#
# NO PRESTIGE. He is not doing something admirable. He is doing something he
# has been made to do by a number, and the mod does not reward arithmetic.
#
# bc_war_is_long_trigger RAISES IT RATHER THAN LOWERING IT, WHICH IS THE
# OPPOSITE OF NOTHING - the longer the war has run the less of it he can pay
# for, so the pressure to end it today is greater and not smaller.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_short_war = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		gold < 0
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
