			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			gold < -100
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_war_is_long_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_short_war"

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_short_war_toast
			desc = bc_the_short_war_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE NAME HE WAS GIVEN, 2026-08-17.
#
# NEITHER `bastard` NOR `legitimized_bastard` APPEARS ANYWHERE IN THE MOD, in
# a setting where the surname a man was handed at birth is the first thing
# anyone in the array knows about him. Sixty-four commander templates ask what
# he is good at, what he believes, who he is angry with and what his lord has
# on him, and none of them ask what his name is.
#
# NOT A SUBSET OF THE LOWBORN TEMPLATES, AND NOT THE SAME QUESTION. `bastard`
# carries `dynasty_opinion = -15` in AGOT 00_traits.txt:11609, which is a modifier
# on how a dynasty regards one of its own - a bastard is IN the house's blood
# and outside its name, which is the entire problem and is exactly what
# `is_lowborn` is not asking. bc_no_name_at_all and
# bc_knight_no_house_behind_him are about a man with nobody behind him. These
# are about a man with a great deal behind him that will not put his name on
# anything.
#
# THE TWO TRAITS ARE MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE BY DEFINITION, not by our arrangement:
# AGOT 00_traits.txt:11599 lists `legitimized_bastard` in `bastard`'s
# `opposites`, and AGOT's own 00_bastard_effects.txt removes one and adds the
# other in the same block (:1077-1078). So the two gates below cannot both
# be open on the same man and no ordering between them is needed.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The parchment settled it and the men were there before the parchment.
#
# `legitimized_bastard` AND THE WINNING ROW. The law has already given him the
# answer; what it cannot give him is the memory of every man over forty in his
# own host, and a victory is the one argument that does not need his name to
# be on it. So he is winning the way a man wins when a good result is not
# enough and it has to be a tidy one.
#
# `ordered`/`up` AND bc_pursuit, WHICH IS THE POINT. He does not let the win
# get loose. The flanks are covered before they are needed and the chase goes
# out in order, because a sloppy victory is the kind that gets talked about
# afterwards in the wrong words.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE. He is buying something with this and the mod should say so -
# but it is a payment on a debt he did not incur, not a windfall.
#
# `content` HALVES IT. A man who has stopped needing to prove the thing does
# not command this way, and the trait is the mod's shortest way of saying so.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_parchment_and_the_men = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_trait = legitimized_bastard
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_parchment_and_the_men"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_parchment_and_the_men_toast
			desc = bc_the_parchment_and_the_men_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# They already have the reason, and it is not the ground.
#
# `bastard` AND THE LOSING ROW. Every other commander in the mod who is losing
# gets an argument about it afterwards - the terrain, the numbers, the hour,
# the man opposite. This one will not, because the explanation was available
# before the battle started and it is about him rather than about the day.
#
# `measured`/`down`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING. There is no army modifier for
# this. It does not make the line braver or steadier or looser; it makes the
# afternoon mean something different to the man in the middle of it, and the
# faltering rung with the stress is the whole of the cost.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT HAVE ANYONE SAY IT OUT LOUD. Nobody in a host tells the
# commander what they think of his birth while the fighting is on. The point
# is that he does not need to be told.
#
# `paranoid` RAISES IT, WHICH IS THE ONE TRAIT ROW HERE THAT IS NOT ABOUT
# COMMAND. A man who assumes he is being talked about is right this time.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_they_have_their_reason = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_trait = bastard
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial <= 8
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_they_have_their_reason"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_they_have_their_reason_toast
			desc = bc_they_have_their_reason_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The small man, and the hour nobody is laughing.
#
# `dwarf`, WHICH APPEARS NOWHERE ELSE IN THE MOD AND IS ONE OF THE MOST
# LOADED TRAITS IN THE SETTING. A dwarf who holds a host has already won an
# argument nobody in it will admit to having had, and an afternoon that is
# going his way is the only hour of the year when the argument stays won.
#
# THE WINNING ROW ONLY, AND NOT BECAUSE THE LOSING ONE WOULD BE UNKIND. The
# losing version is a template about men blaming a body for a battle, and the
# mod already has bc_they_have_their_reason, which is that moment written
# about a different accident of birth and does not need a second draft.
#
# `adapted`/`up`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING, AND THAT IS THE ARGUMENT. He is not
# winning this by presence - nobody is dressing on him and no line is bracing
# because he rode past. He is winning it by having arranged it, and the mod
# has one KIND that means the commander READ the field, and no army modifier
# belongs to it.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE AND A MOMENTUM RUNG. What he gets out of the afternoon is
# that it happened in front of witnesses.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT MAKE HIM A FIGHTER AND MAY NOT PUT HIM IN THE LINE. It also
# may not have anybody say anything about his height - see the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_small_man = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_trait = dwarf
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 30
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = intellect_good_3
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_small_man"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_small_man_toast
			desc = bc_the_small_man_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE MOD'S OWN VERDICT, READ BACK ONTO THE FIELD, 2026-08-17.
#
# BATTLE CHRONICLES HAS BEEN KEEPING A RECORD OF HOW EVERY COMMANDER FIGHTS
# SINCE PLAN 4 AND HAS NEVER LET HIM CARRY IT INTO A BATTLE. `bc_style_last`
# and `bc_style_streak` are written on the commander at the end of every
# notable battle by bc_style_streak_effect and never cleared; at three in a row
# the reward chain hands him one of AGOT's twelve commander traits. Both the
# variables and the traits were read ONLY by the post-battle chain that writes
# them. Sixty-seven commander templates asked about his martial, his purse, his
# body, his kin, his ground and his enemy, and not one of them asked the thing
# the mod itself had already decided about him.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_long_war OR THE MOMENT-COUNT TEMPLATES. Those ask how long
# THIS war or THIS battle has run. A streak is a fact about the man that was
# true before he mounted this morning and will still be true if he loses today.
#
# THE TWO TEMPLATES BELOW ARE THE TWO HONEST READINGS OF A HABIT and they are
# deliberately not a diagonal of one gate: one reads the streak while it is
# working, the other reads the TRAIT the streak already paid out while it is
# not. `reckless` (00_traits.txt:15518, category commander, opposites
# cautious_leader, min_combat_roll -2 max_combat_roll +3) is the trait AGOT
# gives the max_combat_roll gamble, and it is what a `wild` streak of three
# earns from this mod's own reward chain. A man can hold it without ever having
# had a streak - AGOT hands it out at character creation too - so the two gates
# overlap without either containing the other.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The thing he does, working again.
#
# WINNING, BECAUSE A HABIT THAT IS FAILING IS THE OTHER TEMPLATE. What makes
# this worth a moment is not that he has a style; it is that the host has
# learned it. Men who have followed a commander through two battles of the same
# shape start moving before the order arrives, and that is the only thing on
# this field that no amount of martial buys in an afternoon.
#
# `ordered`/`up` AND bc_pursuit. Not `adapted` - he has adapted to nothing,
# that is the point of him. The army modifier is right for the same reason the
# prose is: the gap between the order and the movement has closed, and an army
# that moves the moment it is told is an army that catches people.
#
# THE STREAK IS READ AS OF THIS MORNING. bc_style_streak_effect runs at combat
# end, so nothing this battle has done is in the number yet, and the template
# is about what he walked in with.
#
# NO STYLE ROW. The template deliberately does not care WHICH way he always
# fights - `bc_style_last` would name it, and naming it would force six
# variants of one paragraph and a desc that contradicts five of them. The
# clause pools already vary the wording; the fact here is repetition itself.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE STYLE, MAY NOT NAME A PREVIOUS BATTLE and may not
# promise a trait - the reward chain decides that at three, out of this
# template's sight, and a toast that hinted at it would be writing a promise
# the budget gate can refuse.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_thing_he_always_does = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_fights_the_same_way_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_thing_he_always_does"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_thing_he_always_does_toast
			desc = bc_the_thing_he_always_does_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The day the habit does not pay.
#
# `reckless` AND LOSING, WHICH IS THE TRAIT'S OWN ARITHMETIC COMING DUE.
# AGOT gives it min_combat_roll -2 and max_combat_roll +3: it does not make a
# man better, it widens what can happen to him, and a losing field is the low
# end of that spread arriving. The mod awards this trait itself for three
# `wild` battles running, so on a good number of characters this template is
# the mod reading its own handwriting back.
#
# NO STREAK ROW, DELIBERATELY. The trait outlives the streak that earned it and
# is also handed out by AGOT at character creation, so gating on both would
# throw away every commander who came by it honestly and would make this a
# strict subset of the template above's gate, which the mod does not allow.
#
# `wild`/`down` AND bc_spent_horse. The verb table sends `wild` either way to
# the same modifier, and it is the right one: what a gamble costs when it fails
# is the horses and the men who went with it, not the line's shape.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GUARDED, the standing shape. Stress rather than a
# prestige loss, because prestige is not what this costs him - the trait's
# reputation is intact and that is precisely the problem.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT KILL HIM AND MAY NOT LOSE THE BATTLE. `bc_side_is_losing`
# is a state, not an outcome, and four other chains own the ending. It also may
# not have him change his mind: `cautious_leader` is this trait's `opposites`
# and the reward chain is the only thing in the mod permitted to make that
# swap, after three battles, not in one afternoon.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_gamble_that_does_not_pay = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_trait = reckless
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_gamble_that_does_not_pay"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_gamble_that_does_not_pay_toast
			desc = bc_the_gamble_that_does_not_pay_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# THE COUNTS THE MOD KEEPS AND NEVER PUTS ON THE FIELD, 2026-08-17.
#
# The previous batch let the commander's STYLE onto the field. Two counters
# were still writing themselves into a save and being read by nothing but the
# chain that writes them: `bc_notable_battles`, his tally of victories, and
# `bc_nemesis_losses`, the depth of the one running grudge the mod tracks.
# The rivalry feature is the larger scandal of the two - it has a variable, a
# relation ladder, a game rule, an eve paragraph and five aftermath toasts, and
# in sixty-nine commander templates `bc_facing_nemesis_trigger` appears ONLY as
# a `factor = 1.5` chance modifier. Not one moment in the mod has ever been
# ABOUT the man across the field. Both templates below are losing-side, which
# is not a coincidence: a tally of wins and a tally of losses owed are both
# most legible on the afternoon they are contradicted.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Five fields won, and this one going the other way.
#
# THE COUNTER MEANS VICTORIES, NOT AFTERNOONS. bc_notable_battle_counter_effect
# is called from the winner's arm of the aftermath chain and nowhere else, so
# the number is a record of wins and the desc may not put a defeat in his past
# on the strength of it. What it may say is that everyone present knows the
# record, which is the actual mechanism here: a host that has been told about
# a man for years is a host that spends the bad hour looking at him.
#
# `ordered`/`down`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING, and that is the template. The
# orders are going out and they are the right orders; they are arriving into a
# field that has stopped answering them. There is no army modifier for a plan
# that is correct and late, and inventing one would say the opposite of what
# the moment says.
#
# LOSING, BECAUSE WINNING IS bc_the_thing_he_always_does. That template reads a
# style streak and is about a habit that is working; this reads a victory tally
# and is about a reputation that is not helping. Neither gate contains the
# other - a man can have won five battles in five different ways, and a
# two-battle streak is not five wins.
#
# STRESS AND A GUARDED FALTERING RUNG, NO PRESTIGE LOSS. The record is intact;
# that is exactly why the afternoon is worse for him than for anybody else on
# the field.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A NUMBER OF BATTLES AND MAY NOT NAME ONE OF THEM. The
# mod holds a count and nothing else - no place, no year, no enemy - and a
# toast that supplies any of that invents a fact no other file agrees with.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_record_does_not_help_him = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_won_many_before_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_record_does_not_help_him"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_record_does_not_help_him_toast
			desc = bc_the_record_does_not_help_him_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man who has done this to him before, doing it again.
#
# THE FIRST MOMENT IN THE MOD THAT IS ABOUT THE MAN ACROSS THE FIELD.
# bc_facing_nemesis_trigger has existed since plan 4 and has been used as a
# `factor = 1.5` on every commander template and as an `is_valid` row on none.
# The rivalry feature could raise a grudge to nemesis over a decade without a
# single moment ever mentioning that the two of them had met before.
#
# BOTH ROWS ARE REQUIRED AND NEITHER IS REDUNDANT. bc_nemesis_debt_deep_trigger
# alone says he is carrying a deep grudge against SOMEBODY; the nemesis test
# alone says the man opposite is the one he carries it against, at any depth,
# including a single loss. Together they say the only thing worth a moment.
#
# LOSING, WHICH IS WHERE THE COUNTER CAME FROM. Every point in
# bc_nemesis_losses was paid for by an afternoon like this one, and if this one
# ends the same way the aftermath chain adds another and may set the relation
# itself. The template is the middle of that arithmetic, not its result, and
# the desc may not announce the result.
#
# `entrenched`/`down` AND bc_shieldwall. Not `bloodied` and not `wild`: what a
# man does in front of the one enemy who has beaten him twice is refuse to be
# moved, and the line where he is standing gets harder for the rest of the
# afternoon. The modifier is the whole consequence and it is the right one -
# holding is not winning, and this template must not look like winning.
#
# FIFTEEN STRESS, THE HIGHER OF THE TWO STEPS THE MOD USES, because it is the
# one losing template in the file where the loss is personal.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT KILL EITHER OF THEM, MAY NOT PUT THEM WITHIN REACH OF EACH
# OTHER, AND MAY NOT SETTLE IT. bc_chronicles.0005 owns the thirty feet between
# a commander and a man he wants, the rivalry chain owns the escalation, and
# the battle is not over. It also may not count the previous meetings aloud -
# the mod holds a number, not a history.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_man_who_has_done_this_before = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_facing_nemesis_trigger = yes
		bc_nemesis_debt_deep_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			bc_foe_leader_commands_in_person_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = vengeful
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_man_who_has_done_this_before"

		add_stress = 15

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_man_who_has_done_this_before_toast
			desc = bc_the_man_who_has_done_this_before_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# THE COUNTRY THE WAR KEPT HIM IN, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE LAST OF THE FOUR LEDGER COUNTERS. Batch fifty-one put the style streak on
# the field, fifty-two the victory tally and the grudge, and `bc_terrain_streak`
# was the one left - written at the end of every notable battle since plan 4,
# read by nothing but the chain that writes it. Between them the four counters
# were the whole of what the mod remembered about a commander, and until these
# three batches a player could fight a twenty-year war without one of them ever
# being mentioned during a battle.
#
# BOTH TEMPLATES BELOW READ THE SAME NUMBER AND ARE ABOUT DIFFERENT PEOPLE.
# The winning one is about him and the ground; the losing one is about the man
# opposite, who has been in the same country for the same three battles and has
# had exactly as long to learn it. That asymmetry is the reason the pair is not
# a plain diagonal: the second is not "familiarity failed", it is "familiarity
# was never his alone".
#
# NEITHER MAY NAME A TERRAIN. bc_terrain_last holds one of six BUCKETS and the
# streak effect's header says so in the imperative. The mod does not know
# whether the last three fields were hills or mountains, only that they were
# the same kind of country.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The third field in the same country, and he has stopped having to look.
#
# WINNING, AND THE MECHANISM IS THE STAFF RATHER THAN THE MAN. What three
# battles in one kind of country buy is not a better commander; it is a set of
# captains and scouts and outriders who have all learned the same lessons at
# the same time and no longer have to be told them. That is why the moment is
# `adapted`/`up`: the field is being READ correctly and quickly, which is the
# one thing that KIND means, and there is no army modifier for it because
# reading is not a thing an army does to the other army.
#
# NOT bc_the_thing_he_always_does. That reads a STYLE streak and is about how
# he fights; this reads a TERRAIN streak and is about where. A man can have
# fought three battles in the same country in three different ways, and a
# two-battle style streak says nothing about the ground. Neither gate contains
# the other.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE AND A GUARDED MOMENTUM RUNG, the standard winning shape.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE COUNTRY, MAY NOT SAY HE CHOSE IT and may not have
# him give a speech about it. The counter says the war kept him here; it does
# not say he wanted to be.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_same_country_again = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_same_ground_again_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = strategist
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_same_country_again"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_same_country_again_toast
			desc = bc_the_same_country_again_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The other man has been here just as long.
#
# THE SAME COUNTER, AND NOT THE SAME TEMPLATE INVERTED. The obvious losing
# version of the one above is "his familiarity did not save him", which is
# bc_the_record_does_not_help_him wearing different clothes. This one is about
# the fact the winning template quietly assumes and never checks: a war that
# has kept him in one kind of country for three battles has kept the man
# opposite in it too, and the ground teaches both sides at the same rate. The
# subject is the enemy, which no other template gated on a ledger counter has
# been.
#
# `adapted`/`down`, THE THINNEST CELL IN THE PAIR CENSUS AND THE EXACT ONE.
# `adapted` is the KIND that means the field was READ, and this is a moment
# about being out-read on ground he thought he owned. It hands out nothing,
# correctly - being anticipated is not a casualty modifier and not a break in
# the line, it is a plan arriving where somebody is already standing.
#
# A GUARDED FALTERING RUNG AND TEN STRESS, no prestige loss. Nothing has gone
# wrong with his army; something has gone right with the other one.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE COUNTRY OR THE ENEMY. The bucket is not a terrain
# and `bc_battle_foe` is not read here - the man opposite may be a stranger,
# and bc_the_man_who_has_done_this_before owns the case where he is not.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_he_learned_it_too = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_same_ground_again_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 40
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_foe_is_formidable_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_he_learned_it_too"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_he_learned_it_too_toast
			desc = bc_he_learned_it_too_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# THE GODS ON THE FIELD, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE MOD HAS ASKED ABOUT FAITH EXACTLY ONCE AND ONLY COMPARATIVELY.
# `bc_foe_is_heathen_trigger` reads `NOT = { faith = root.faith }` on the man
# opposite; nothing anywhere reads what a commander or a knight actually
# believes. In a setting whose armies are told apart by their gods more
# reliably than by their banners, seventy-three commander templates and
# forty-eight knight templates had no opinion on the subject.
#
# `has_religion = religion:X`, NOT `faith = faith:X`, AND THIS IS THE WHOLE
# TECHNICAL POINT OF THE BATCH. AGOT splits every one of its religions into
# several faiths - the Seven alone ships `fots_seven` and `fots_blessed` and
# more, R'hllor ships `rhllor_fc` and `rhllor_fots`, the old gods ship
# `old_gods_wnw` and `old_gods_btw_fnf` - so a gate written against one faith
# key silently drops every worshipper of the same gods who happens to belong to
# a different branch of them. The religion is the level at which "he keeps
# these gods" is true, and `has_religion` is the documented character trigger
# for it in the 1.19.0.6 dump. Nothing below names a faith.
#
# THE THREE RELIGIONS ARE AGOT'S OWN, verified in its religion_types folder:
# `the_ways_religion` holds the drowned_god faith (00_agot_the_ways.txt:219),
# `the_rhllor_religion` is the Lord of Light, and `the_pact_religion` holds
# old_gods_wnw (00_agot_the_pact.txt:213). This mod defines no religions and
# reads three of the twenty-two.
#
# NONE OF THE THREE TEMPLATES IS ABOUT THEOLOGY AND ALL THREE ARE ABOUT WHAT
# THE MAN NEXT TO HIM EXPECTS. A god only reaches a battlefield through what
# everybody present assumes will happen next, and that is the only mechanism
# any of these descs is allowed to use.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The men who do not think losing is the worst of it.
#
# LOSING, BECAUSE THIS FAITH HAS NOTHING PARTICULAR TO SAY ABOUT WINNING. Every
# host in the world holds together while it is winning; what the drowned god's
# men are famous for is the other hour, and a template that fired on a good
# afternoon would be describing something no more ironborn than anyone else.
#
# `entrenched`/`down` AND bc_shieldwall, WHICH IS THE ENTIRE CONSEQUENCE. Not
# `bloodied`: they are not killing more of the other side, they are declining
# to move, and the modifier for a line that has stopped receding is exactly the
# one this KIND hands out. The moment must not look like a rally -
# bc_rally_the_line owns that and this is not it. Nothing here reverses.
#
# NO PRESTIGE AND A GUARDED FALTERING RUNG. He is still losing. The point of
# the moment is what it costs the other host to keep winning.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT QUOTE THE CREED, MAY NOT DROWN ANYBODY AND MAY NOT PROMISE
# THEY HOLD. It is a state, not an outcome, and the aftermath chain owns
# outcomes.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_they_do_not_ask_for_terms = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_religion = religion:the_ways_religion
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = zealous
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_they_do_not_ask_for_terms"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_they_do_not_ask_for_terms_toast
			desc = bc_they_do_not_ask_for_terms_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A host that has decided its god is winning.
#
# WINNING, AND IT IS THE ONLY SIDE OF THIS RELIGION WORTH A MOMENT. A losing
# host of the Lord of Light is a losing host; a winning one is the specific
# problem of an army that has stopped needing to be told to go forward, which
# is not a good thing and is not written here as one.
#
# `wild`/`up` AND bc_spent_horse. The verb table sends `wild` to the same
# modifier in both directions and that is exactly right here: this is a moment
# about a host spending itself, and the fact that it is spending itself in the
# direction of a victory does not make the spending cheaper. A commander who
# reads this toast and thinks it is unambiguously good has read it correctly -
# so has one who thinks it is a warning.
#
# NO RUNG, DELIBERATELY. Momentum is the mod's word for a side's standing
# improving, and nothing here improves it: the men are already going forward.
# Granting a rung would say the commander achieved this, and the whole template
# is about a thing happening slightly out of his hands. Precedent for a
# rung-less template is bc_knight_will_not_be_carried and now bc_knight_the_spurs_they_took.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT PERFORM A MIRACLE, MAY NOT BURN ANYONE AND MAY NOT PUT A
# PRIEST IN COMMAND. The red priests are a fact of this religion; the man
# holding the army together is still the commander, and the mod has no way to
# put anybody else there.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_fire_is_ahead_of_him = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		has_religion = religion:the_rhllor_religion
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			has_trait = zealous
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = cynical
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_fire_is_ahead_of_him"

		add_prestige = 50

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_fire_is_ahead_of_him_toast
			desc = bc_the_fire_is_ahead_of_him_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# THE WAR THIS BATTLE BELONGS TO, 2026-08-17.
#
# In the whole mod the war scope has been read ONCE. bc_war_is_long_trigger
# walks `scope:combat_side.combat.combat_war`, asks `war_days >= 730`, and
# that is the entire extent of the mod's interest in the quarrel the battle is
# an afternoon of. The 1.19.0.6 dump offers `is_civil_war`, `is_war_leader`,
# `attacker_war_score`, `defender_war_score` and `days_since_max_war_score` on
# the same scope, all of them free, all of them reachable since plan 1.
#
# TWO OF THOSE ARE WORTH A MOMENT AND THREE ARE NOT. The war scores need to
# know which side of the war this combat side is on before they mean anything,
# and the mod has no clean way to ask that; they are left alone deliberately
# rather than guessed at. `is_civil_war` and `is_war_leader` need nothing but
# the war and the man, and both of them change what a losing hour MEANS rather
# than how bad it is, which is the only kind of context this mod is for.
#
# BOTH TEMPLATES ARE LOSING-SIDE. Winning does not ask a commander who these
# people are or whose name is on the quarrel. Losing does both.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A CIVIL WAR IS THE ONE KIND OF BATTLE WHERE BOTH HOSTS CAME OUT OF THE SAME
# REALM, and the engine will tell us so for nothing. bc_war_is_a_rebellion_trigger
# is `is_civil_war` on the war behind this combat, guarded the way every war
# read in this mod is guarded.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_same_tongue. That one gates on
# bc_foe_is_countryman_trigger - the two commanders share a culture - and a
# civil war implies nothing of the kind: the Reach and the Iron Islands can
# rebel against the same king in the same war without sharing a word of
# anything. Neither gate is a subset of the other in either direction.
#
# `bloodied`/`down` AND THEREFORE bc_bloodied. The verb table sends bloodied
# both ways to the same modifier and that is the point being made: this hour
# costs what it costs, and the reason it costs more than an ordinary bad hour
# is that the men in front of him are not strangers and neither host is
# willing to be the one that breaks first in front of the other.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A HOUSE, A KING OR A CLAIM. The gate knows the war is
# internal and knows nothing else - not who rose, not against whom, not what
# for. It may not say "brother" or "cousin" literally either; the mod has not
# checked a single relation here.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_other_half_of_the_realm = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_war_is_a_rebellion_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_war_is_long_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_other_half_of_the_realm"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_other_half_of_the_realm_toast
			desc = bc_the_other_half_of_the_realm_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# `is_war_leader` IS READ ON THE WAR AND TAKES THE CHARACTER AS ITS TARGET -
# "is the target character leading one of the sides in the scope war". So
# bc_the_war_is_his_trigger walks out to the war and compares back against
# `root`, which in a commander phase event's is_valid is the commander; this
# file already reads root that way in bc_foe_is_countryman_trigger and
# bc_foe_is_heathen_trigger.
#
# THE TRIGGER IS FOR THE COMMANDER POOL ONLY. From a knight template `root` is
# the knight, who is essentially never a war leader, and the answer would be a
# uselessly rare no. It is not used there and should not be.
#
# IT DOES NOT MEAN HE IS THE GREAT MAN. A war leader can be a rebel with one
# county or a claimant with none. What it means is that the quarrel is HIS:
# there is nobody further up the chain to carry the blame for this afternoon,
# nobody to appeal to, and whatever gets signed at the end of it will be
# signed by him. That is a different bad hour from losing somebody else's
# battle, and it is the only thing this template is about.
#
# `wild`/`down` AND bc_spent_horse. A man with nobody above him and a battle
# going the wrong way commits everything he has, because holding something in
# reserve is only worth doing if there is a later that somebody else is
# responsible for.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE WAR, THE ENEMY OR THE TERMS, and may not have him
# decide anything - he is not surrendering in this toast, he is discovering
# that there is no one to ask.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_his_own_war = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_the_war_is_his_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_his_own_war"

		add_stress = 15

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_his_own_war_toast
			desc = bc_his_own_war_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# THE BODY THAT ARRIVED, NOT THE BODY THE BATTLE MADE, 2026-08-17.
#
# The body batch and plan 6's wound both ask the same question - what did the
# fighting do to him - and between them they own six gates. Nothing in sixty
# batches has asked the other question, which is what he brought with him. In
# a setting where more captains are finished by a fever in a tent than by
# anything with an edge on it, the mod has had no opinion on a commander who
# was already sick when the horns went.
#
# THE GATE IS bc_he_is_unwell_trigger, `has_trait_with_flag = illness`, which
# covers sixteen AGOT traits and will cover the seventeenth for free.
#
# NEITHER TEMPLATE MAY BE A DEATHBED. The mod does not kill anybody in a phase
# event and is not going to start; both of these are about a man doing his job
# through something, and both are careful to leave him doing it.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A NOTE THAT BELONGS ON bc_he_cannot_get_there AND IS PUT HERE BECAUSE THIS
# IS THE BATCH THAT FOUND IT. `_traits.info:75` defines
# `disables_combat_leadership` as "this character is blocked from being a
# commander", and wounded_1, wounded_2 and wounded_3 all set it in AGOT's
# 00_traits.txt. So a man cannot be GIVEN an army while he is carrying a wound
# trait, and bc_he_cannot_get_there is reachable only where the wound arrives
# after he already has one - vanilla's own combat injuries and plan 6's wound
# effect, both of which fire on a man who is already commanding. That template
# is not dead and its comment is not wrong, but it is much rarer than its
# `base = 40` suggests, and the reason is a field in a trait file rather than
# anything in this mod. Whether the engine also pulls a commander who becomes
# wounded mid-combat is not knowable without a run and is on the sweep list.
#
# NONE OF THE SIXTEEN ILLNESS TRAITS SETS THAT FIELD, which is checked in the
# trigger's own header. A sick man may be handed an army; that is the whole
# reason these two templates exist and the wound pair could not be written
# this way.
#
# THIS ONE IS THE LOSING HALF AND IT IS NOT ABOUT PAIN. He is not brave here
# and he is not suffering picturesquely; he is making the cheaper decision
# every time, because the expensive decision costs him something he does not
# have this afternoon, and the men pay the difference.
#
# `bloodied`/`down` AND bc_bloodied, WHICH IS THE POINT. The verb table's
# bloodied row is casualties, and casualties are exactly what a commander buys
# when he stops manoeuvring and starts closing because closing is shorter.
#
# NOT A SUBSET OF ANYTHING. bc_he_cannot_get_there is the wound ladder and no
# illness trait is a wound trait; bc_the_gamble_that_does_not_pay is `reckless`
# and this man is doing the opposite of gambling.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE ILLNESS. Sixteen traits share this gate and they
# have nothing in common but that he is unwell - no cough, no rash, no fever
# named, nothing a maester would write down.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_shorter_of_the_two_orders = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_he_is_unwell_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			age >= 50
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			bc_host_is_vast_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = strategist
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_shorter_of_the_two_orders"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_shorter_of_the_two_orders_toast
			desc = bc_the_shorter_of_the_two_orders_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE WINNING HALF, AND THE REASON IT IS NOT THE SAME TEMPLATE TWICE. A sick
# man losing spends the army because it is quicker. A sick man winning spends
# nothing at all: he rides to one place, stays there, and gives about a third
# of the orders he would normally give - and a great many of the orders a
# commander normally gives are only there to prove he is still watching.
#
# `measured`/`up`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING, AND THAT IS CORRECT. There is no
# army modifier that means "he judged it well", and there should not be one:
# what happened here is that he could not afford to be anywhere else, and it
# turned out that the field did not need him anywhere else. The rung is the
# whole reward, and it is guarded like every other momentum grant.
#
# `measured`/`up` IS ALSO THE RAREST PAIR IN THE MOD - four sites before this
# one. It is rare because it needs a moment where the right thing happened and
# the army did not have to do anything to get it, which is a narrow shape.
# This is one.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE ILLNESS, MAY NOT HAVE HIM ALMOST FALL AND MAY NOT
# HAVE ANYONE FUSS OVER HIM. The second is a deathbed by another route; the
# third puts the moment in somebody else's hands, and this template is about a
# man deciding to spend nothing.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_he_does_not_move_from_the_rise = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_he_is_unwell_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = impatient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_he_does_not_move_from_the_rise"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_he_does_not_move_from_the_rise_toast
			desc = bc_he_does_not_move_from_the_rise_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# THE RUNGS, READ BACK AS GATES, 2026-08-17.
#
# The momentum and faltering ladders are the mod's own model of how a battle
# is going, they are written by nearly every template in both files, and in
# sixty-two batches they have been READ as a gate exactly once -
# bc_rally_the_line, which asks for faltering because a rally needs a setback
# to reverse. Momentum has never gated anything at all. The mod has been
# keeping a running opinion about the afternoon and never once asking itself
# what it thinks.
#
# THE FALTERING HALF IS CLOSED FOR THE COMMANDER POOL AND THIS IS NOT A
# JUDGEMENT CALL. bc_rally_the_line's is_valid is the ready trigger and
# `bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes`, nothing else, so ANY commander template
# gated on faltering plus one more row is a strict subset of it by
# construction. The faltering read therefore goes in the knight pool, which
# rolls from a different list and cannot compete with it.
#
# NEITHER TEMPLATE MAY GRANT A RUNG, AND THE REASON IS STRUCTURAL RATHER THAN
# EDITORIAL. Every momentum grant in this mod is wrapped in
# `NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes }` and every faltering grant in
# `NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }`. A template that GATES on the rung
# has already made its own guard false, so the grant would be dead code that
# reads as if it did something. The gate and the guard are exact complements;
# writing both is a contradiction with a `base` on it.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE ARMY ALREADY HAS A RUNG AND THE AFTERNOON IS STILL GOING ITS WAY. That
# is a different fact from winning, and the difference is time: the side row
# says how the fighting stands right now, and the modifier says that something
# earlier today went well enough for the mod to write it down. A host that is
# ahead is not the same animal as a host that has been ahead for a while.
#
# NOT A SUBSET OF ANY SHIPPED TEMPLATE. No commander template in this file is
# gated on a side row alone - that was checked mechanically over both files
# before this was written - so `momentum + winning` is not a narrowing of
# anything that exists.
#
# `ordered`/`up` AND bc_pursuit, THE MODIFIER THAT MEANS THEY ARE CHASING.
# This is the one moment in the mod where that is unambiguously the right verb:
# the men are not being asked to hold anything or to spend anything, they are
# being asked to keep doing what has already worked twice, and the only thing
# an army needs for that is permission to move faster.
#
# PRESTIGE INSTEAD OF A RUNG, per the section header - the grant would be dead
# code. 50, the file's standard, not more: this is the afternoon paying out,
# not a new achievement.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT WIN THE BATTLE, MAY NOT ROUT ANYBODY AND MAY NOT NAME THE
# ENEMY COMMANDER. The mod does not end battles in phase events, and a rout is
# the aftermath's word.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_they_have_the_shape_of_it_now = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_side_is_outnumbering_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = strategist
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_they_have_the_shape_of_it_now"

		add_prestige = 50

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_they_have_the_shape_of_it_now_toast
			desc = bc_they_have_the_shape_of_it_now_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# THE TWO ARMIES AT THE EDGE OF THE MAP, 2026-08-17.
#
# EVERY TEMPLATE IN SIXTY-FOUR BATCHES HAS ASSUMED A FEUDAL ARMY. Lords,
# knights, oaths that come with land, men who can be ransomed, a chain of
# command that ends in somebody with a seat. AGOT ships two hosts that are not
# that at all, they fight each other constantly, and the mod has never once
# noticed either of them.
#
# THE PAIR IS ABOUT WHAT HOLDS AN ARMY TOGETHER WHEN THE USUAL THING IS ABSENT.
# The Watch has a vow and no exit - discharge is death, desertion is death, and
# the man beside you came here instead of a rope. The free folk have an exit
# and nothing else - nobody is sworn, nobody is owed, and a man follows the man
# he picked for exactly as long as he still thinks it was a good pick. They are
# the same question answered in opposite directions, which is why they are one
# batch, and both take the losing side because that is the hour the answer
# gets tested.
#
# NEITHER TEMPLATE MAY BE A LAST STAND. The mod does not end battles in phase
# events and does not kill anybody in them; both of these are afternoons in the
# middle of something.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE WATCH, LOSING. bc_sworn_to_the_wall_trigger wraps AGOT's own set of three
# traits, which matters here more than usual - a great many of the black
# brothers a player will ever see on a field are `nightswatch_historical` from
# a bookmark or `nightswatch_temp` from AGOT's mega-war content, and a bare
# `has_trait = nightswatch` would have quietly excluded them.
#
# NOT A SUBSET OF ANYTHING SHIPPED. No commander template is gated on a side
# row alone, checked mechanically over both files, and nothing in the mod has
# ever read an order membership - bc_is_kingsguard_trigger is the one
# comparable gate and it lives in the knight pool.
#
# `entrenched`/`down` AND bc_shieldwall. The modifier is the correct one and
# the reason is not courage: a line that cannot leave gets closer together,
# because the only two things available to it are standing still and standing
# still in a tighter formation. The template is careful not to call that
# bravery, and the desc is under the same instruction.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED, GUARDED, LIKE EVERY OTHER LOSING TEMPLATE.
# Holding is not winning and this side is still going backwards.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT QUOTE THE OATH, NAME A CASTLE, OR PUT WILDLINGS ACROSS THE
# FIELD. The gate says who these men are and says nothing whatever about who
# they are fighting - the Watch has been dragged into southron wars often
# enough that assuming the enemy is beyond the Wall would be inventing a fact.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_there_is_nowhere_to_desert_to = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_sworn_to_the_wall_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_there_is_nowhere_to_desert_to"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_there_is_nowhere_to_desert_to_toast
			desc = bc_there_is_nowhere_to_desert_to_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# WHOSE GROUND THIS IS, LEGALLY, 2026-08-17.
#
# Five triggers ask what the battlefield is MADE OF and none has ever asked
# who it BELONGS TO. That is a strange gap for a mod set in a civil war -
# `bc_war_is_a_rebellion_trigger` landed five batches ago and the whole point
# of a rebellion is that both hosts are standing on somebody's land who is
# present and can see it.
#
# THE TWO HALVES ARE NOT THE SAME MOMENT WITH THE SIDE ROW FLIPPED. Winning on
# your own ground is an advantage made of knowledge - who knows the ditches,
# who can be asked, where the roads actually go. Losing on it is a bill, and
# the bill arrives whichever way the afternoon ends. Those are different facts
# about the same county and they are written as different templates rather
# than one template with two toasts.
#
# NEITHER MAY BURN A VILLAGE ON THE PLAYER'S BEHALF or move a single number in
# a county. The mod does not touch the map and these do not start.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE WINNING HALF, WHICH IS ABOUT KNOWING THINGS. Everybody on this side who
# grew up within thirty miles of here is worth a scout today, and a good many
# of them are standing in the line.
#
# NOT bc_the_same_country_again. That one reads `bc_terrain_streak` - the
# campaign has kept him in the same KIND of country for three notable battles -
# and it is true of a man four hundred miles from anything he owns. This one is
# about the deed. Neither implies the other and the descs are pointed in
# different directions on purpose: the terrain-streak toast is about a habit,
# this one is about a place.
#
# `adapted`/`up`, WHICH FALLS THROUGH THE VERB TABLE AND HANDS OUT NOTHING.
# That is the correct answer twice over. There is no army modifier meaning "the
# men know the country", and inventing one would make local knowledge into
# combat strength, which is exactly the mistake the modifier set was built to
# avoid - bc_pursuit would say they are chasing and bc_shieldwall would say
# they have braced, and neither has happened.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS THE WHOLE REWARD, guarded as always, plus the file's
# standard 50 prestige.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A HOLDING, A VILLAGE OR A RIVER. The gate knows the
# county is inside his realm and nothing else about it - not its name, not what
# is on it, not whether he has ever been here before.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_every_ditch_here_has_a_name = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_the_ground_is_his_own_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = diligent
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_every_ditch_here_has_a_name"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_every_ditch_here_has_a_name_toast
			desc = bc_every_ditch_here_has_a_name_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE LOSING HALF, WHICH IS A BILL. Whatever this field is worth, it was worth
# it to him this morning, and it is being spent either way.
#
# `bloodied`/`down` AND bc_bloodied. Casualties are the honest verb: a man
# losing on ground he owns keeps feeding the fight for longer than the odds
# justify, because the alternative is not a shorter battle but the same battle
# happening eight miles further into his own country.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED, GUARDED. Nothing here reverses anything.
#
# NOT A SUBSET OF bc_the_other_half_of_the_realm - a civil war is not implied
# by owning the county and owning the county is not implied by a civil war -
# and not a subset of the terrain templates, which cannot see a deed.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT PUT A NUMBER ON WHAT IT COSTS, may not name a holding, and
# may not have him regret giving battle. He chose this field; the toast is
# about what choosing it is turning out to mean, not about him wishing he had
# not.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_it_is_his_own_country_underfoot = {

# ===========================================================================
# WHO WALKED ONTO WHOM, 2026-08-17.
#
# The oldest triggers in the mod compare the two sides' strengths, and every
# batch since has layered facts on top of that comparison - terrain, war,
# faith, deed, opinion. None of them has ever asked the first question anybody
# standing on a battlefield would ask, which is whether this side came here or
# was found here.
#
# THE TWO TEMPLATES ARE THE TWO HALVES WHERE IT MATTERS MOST. An attack that
# is failing and a defence that is holding are the two states in which the
# choice made this morning is still visibly running the afternoon; an attack
# that is working and a defence that is failing are both just the battle going
# the way the numbers said, and neither would say anything the side row does
# not already say.
#
# NEITHER MAY DISENGAGE, WITHDRAW OR CALL ANYTHING OFF. The mod does not end
# battles in a phase event, and "he broke off the attack" is exactly that.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# HE CAME HERE, AND IT IS NOT WORKING. The distinctive thing about a failing
# attack is not that it is going badly - the side row covers that - it is that
# every part of the plan was his, so there is nobody to blame for the ground,
# the hour or the shape of it, and the men know that too.
#
# `wild`/`down` AND bc_spent_horse. An attack that stops working does not turn
# into a defence; it turns into the same attack made again, and then again,
# because stopping is an admission and each repetition is cheaper to order than
# it is to make. That is precisely what the wild row means - a host spending
# itself - and the modifier lands on the right side of the field.
#
# NOT A SUBSET OF bc_the_gamble_that_does_not_pay, which is `reckless` and a
# fact about the man; a cautious commander attacks too, and a reckless one gets
# attacked. Neither gate implies the other.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT HAVE HIM ORDER A WITHDRAWAL, may not name the objective, and
# may not put a number on the attempts. The gate knows he came here and that it
# is going badly, and nothing at all about what he came for.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_he_chose_the_hour = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_his_side_attacked_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_he_chose_the_hour"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_he_chose_the_hour_toast
			desc = bc_he_chose_the_hour_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THEY CAME TO HIM AND IT IS GOING BADLY FOR THEM. The mirror image, and the
# quietest good hour in the mod: he has not done anything today. He picked a
# place, he stood on it, and the other man's plan has been arriving in pieces
# ever since.
#
# `entrenched`/`up` AND bc_shieldwall, WHICH IS THE ONE MOMENT THAT MODIFIER
# WAS ALWAYS FOR. A side that is being attacked and winning is doing exactly
# one thing - not moving - and that is the whole of what bc_shieldwall means.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED, GUARDED, AND THE PRESTIGE IS THE FILE'S
# STANDARD 50. Not more: a defence that works is worth less in reputation than
# an attack that works, which is unfair and is also how it has always been
# recorded.
#
# NOT A SUBSET OF THE TERRAIN TEMPLATES. bc_terrain_favours_us_trigger is about
# what the ground is; this is about who moved. A man can be attacked on bad
# ground and choose good ground to attack across.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT COUNTERATTACK, may not have the enemy break, and may not
# make him clever. The whole moment is that the interesting decisions today
# were all made by somebody else and none of them worked.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_they_came_to_him = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes

		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_terrain_favours_us_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = impatient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander

			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_they_came_to_him_toast
			desc = bc_they_came_to_him_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
