# ===========================================================================
# WATER ON THE FIELD, 2026-08-17.
#
# The mod has six terrain bands, a winter gate and a corpse gate, and in
# sixty-seven batches nothing has ever noticed a river. That is a peculiar
# omission for Westeros in particular, where the wars are fought in the
# Riverlands as a matter of geography and the battles are named after fords.
#
# A RIVER IS NOT TERRAIN AND COULD NOT HAVE BEEN CAUGHT BY THE BANDS. CK3
# carries it as a flag on the province, the six bands partition AGOT's terrain
# helpers exhaustively, and `floodplains` and `wetlands` - the nearest things
# in the partition - both mean soft going rather than a bank with two crossings
# on it. The gate had to be a new one and it is in bc_scripted_triggers.txt
# rather than the ground file for the reason set out there.
#
# ONE TEMPLATE IN EACH POOL, AND THE SPLIT IS THE USUAL ONE. From the saddle a
# river is a constraint on where the battle can be; from the line it is
# something a man is standing in.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE COMMANDER'S HALF, LOSING. A riverside field has two or three places where
# anything can happen and both hosts worked out which ones before noon. That is
# an advantage while it is working and a trap the moment it is not, because a
# battle that has been funnelled cannot be un-funnelled by the man who is
# losing it.
#
# `bloodied`/`down` AND bc_bloodied. The verb is casualties and the reason is
# geometry rather than courage: the same number of men are trying to fight
# along a third of the frontage, nobody can be withdrawn from a crossing
# without the crossing being lost, and a compressed fight kills at a rate an
# open one does not.
#
# NOT THE ENTRENCHED ARGUMENT, DELIBERATELY. bc_there_is_nowhere_to_desert_to
# is a line that cannot leave and gets bc_shieldwall for it; that is a fact
# about the men. This is a fact about the ground, the men can leave perfectly
# well, and what is happening to them is arithmetic.
#
# NOT A SUBSET OF THE TERRAIN TEMPLATES. The six bands are about what the
# ground is made of and this is about what is running through it - a riverside
# field can be open, rough, forest or winter, and the gates do not overlap in
# either direction.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE RIVER, may not have anybody drown, and may not put
# a bridge on the field. The gate says the province has a river in it and says
# nothing about how anyone gets across.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_it_all_comes_down_to_the_crossings = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_field_is_riverside_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_host_is_vast_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = impatient
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = strategist
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_it_all_comes_down_to_the_crossings"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_it_all_comes_down_to_the_crossings_toast
			desc = bc_it_all_comes_down_to_the_crossings_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE EDGE THAT CANNOT BE TURNED, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE MOD HAD NEVER LOOKED AT THE WATER. Batch sixty-three found the river and
# read it as a thing running ACROSS the field - crossings, frontage, footing.
# The coast is the other water fact and it is the opposite shape: the sea is
# at ONE EDGE, and an edge is a flank that does not exist. Nobody rides around
# it, nobody rolls it up, and every reserve the enemy has must be spent on the
# frontage rather than on the end of the line.
#
# TWO TEMPLATES, AND THEY DO NOT SHARE AN ARGUMENT. The commander's is
# arithmetic - he is holding a shorter line than the field is wide and he
# knows it. The knight's is the same edge from the waterline, where a horse is
# a liability and mail is worse than a liability, and it goes in the knight
# file because it needs a man in the shallows rather than a man reading them.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The flank the enemy cannot buy. Coastal, and the side is winning.
#
# `bc_field_is_coastal_trigger`, WHICH IS NEW AND IS NOT THE RIVER GATE. Both
# can be true of the same province and both are province flags rather than
# terrain bands, but they are different tactical facts and this template is
# only ever about the edge. See the trigger's header.
#
# `measured`/`up`, THE RAREST PAIR IN THE MOD AND THE ONE THIS ACTUALLY IS. He
# is not spending anything to hold the coast flank; the coast holds it. What
# he has is men he did not have to commit, which is precisely what `measured`
# means in the ledger - husbanded rather than won. The verb table hands out
# nothing for it, which is right: no army modifier belongs to an accident of
# the map, and the fifty prestige plus the momentum rung are the whole reward.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GUARDED because a man who already has one does not get
# a second, and the gate does not check for the rung, so the grant is live.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT PUT SHIPS ON THE WATER and may not have anybody land behind
# either host - that is a different battle and the gate did not check for it.
# See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_sea_holds_that_flank = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_field_is_coastal_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Seeing that the map has done half the work for you is a thing you
		# either do at first light or never.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = strategist
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = impatient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_sea_holds_that_flank"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_sea_holds_that_flank_toast
			desc = bc_the_sea_holds_that_flank_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE MEN WHO ARE HERE FOR MONEY, 2026-08-17.
#
# EVERY COMMANDER IN THIS MOD HAS BEEN A LORD SO FAR. Sixty-eight batches
# asked about his ground, his faith, his war, his body, his oath and his
# opinion of the man next to him, and all of them assumed the host behind him
# was made of people who owe him something. AGOT is not short of the other
# kind, and CK3 marks it plainly: `government_is_mercenary`, which the trigger
# header quotes AGOT's own scripts on.
#
# THE ARGUMENT BOTH TEMPLATES RUN ON. A lord who loses two thousand men has
# lost two thousand of his own smallfolk and will feel it at harvest. A
# captain who loses two thousand men has lost the thing he sells. That single
# difference changes what a bad hour looks like from the front and what a good
# one is FOR, and it is the only thing in the mod that distinguishes a host
# from an army.
#
# ONE COMMANDER MOMENT AND ONE KNIGHT MOMENT, AND THE KNIGHT'S GATE IS A WALK.
# The captain has the government; a man riding in his company may or may not
# be carrying it himself, so the knight template asks the commander instead,
# on the pattern bc_knight_nothing_is_coming_from_behind established.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# They are not his smallfolk. A free company, and the side is losing.
#
# `ordered`/`down`, WHICH IS THE POINT AND NOT A HEDGE. A levy that starts
# going backwards comes apart, because the men in it have somewhere else to
# be and no reason to be here after the reason stops working. A company that
# comes apart has destroyed its own value, so it does not come apart - it goes
# backwards in step, which is a thing that has to be taught and paid for and
# is exactly what the buyer thought he was buying. The verb table hands out
# nothing for `ordered`/`down`, and that is correct twice over: bc_pursuit is
# for a side chasing, and a company holding its shape while losing is not
# earning an army modifier, it is refusing to spend one.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS STILL GRANTED. Keeping shape is not winning, and the
# ledger should say the afternoon went against him. The gate does not check
# for the rung, so the guarded grant is live.
#
# TEN STRESS AND NO PRESTIGE. The company will be paid whatever happens; what
# he is losing today is stock, and nobody hands a man prestige for the tidiness
# of a defeat.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT TAKE THE COMPANY OFF THE FIELD. A phase event does not end
# a battle, and a captain who has withdrawn is not in the chronicle any more.
# See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_they_are_not_his_smallfolk = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_he_leads_a_free_company_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The arithmetic gets louder the further behind the numbers already
		# are.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = cynical
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = zealous
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_they_are_not_his_smallfolk"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_they_are_not_his_smallfolk_toast
			desc = bc_they_are_not_his_smallfolk_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# WHAT THE MAN ACROSS THE FIELD IS WORTH, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE MOD ASKS SEVEN QUESTIONS ABOUT THE ENEMY COMMANDER AND ALL SEVEN ARE
# ABOUT WHO HE IS OR HOW GOOD HE IS. Kin, house, culture, faith, martial,
# outmatched, present in person - his blood, his tongue, his god and his skill.
# Not one of them asks what he is WORTH, and the bond ladder cannot answer it
# either: a king and a hedge knight sort into the same bucket on all five of
# its arms. Whether there is a crown on the other side of the field is the
# largest single fact about an afternoon in Westeros and the chronicle has been
# silent on it.
#
# SEE THE TRIGGER HEADER for why the answer is `enemy_side` read fresh rather
# than var:bc_battle_foe, which is the rule the other seven follow too.
#
# THE COMMANDER TAKES THE WINNING HALF AND THE KNIGHT THE LOSING ONE, which is
# the reverse of bc_the_crown_on_the_field two thousand lines above - that is a
# man losing with his OWN crown on. The two never compete: they read different
# characters, they take opposite side rows, and they roll from separate pools.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The other banner is a king's. Facing a crown, and the side is winning.
#
# `ordered`/`up` AND bc_pursuit, WHICH IS THE ONE MOMENT THAT MODIFIER IS MOST
# OBVIOUSLY FOR. When an ordinary host breaks, a pursuit is a thousand men
# looking for a thousand different things. When a king's host breaks, every man
# on this side is looking for exactly one banner, and an army that all wants
# the same thing at the same time is the definition of a pursuit that works.
#
# IT MAY NOT CATCH HIM AND THE TEMPLATE DOES NOT PRETEND OTHERWISE. A phase
# event does not end a battle, take a prisoner or kill anybody; what the side
# gets is the modifier and the rung, and what happens to the king happens in
# the aftermath system or not at all.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE AND A GUARDED MOMENTUM RUNG. The gate reads the enemy's rank
# and the side row, and nothing about this commander's own rungs, so the grant
# is live.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A KINGDOM, may not put the king in reach, and may not
# say he is fleeing - the host is going, which is not the same claim. See the
# loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_other_banner_is_a_kings = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_facing_a_crown_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who is nobody in particular has more to gain from the hour than
		# a man who already outranks half the field.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			highest_held_title_tier <= tier_county
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_other_banner_is_a_kings"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_other_banner_is_a_kings_toast
			desc = bc_the_other_banner_is_a_kings_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE MAN WHO IS NOT ENTIRELY HERE, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE CHRONICLE HAS READ THE WHOLE BODY AND NEVER THE MIND. Wounds, maiming,
# one eye, greyscale, physique good and bad, illness, age, and stress - and
# stress is not this. Stress is what the afternoon does to him; this was true
# before anybody drew a sword. In a setting whose central house is defined by
# the question, that is a strange gap.
#
# BOTH MOMENTS TAKE THE LOSING ROW AND THE REASON IS THAT THE OTHER HALF IS
# ALREADY WRITTEN. bc_the_wrath_takes_him and bc_knight_the_glory_hound are
# both `wild`/`up` and both are already the moment where a man who should not
# be trusted with a decision makes one and it works. Writing madness as useful
# would be those two templates a third and fourth time under a different gate.
# What is unwritten is madness as a thing the afternoon has to work AROUND -
# an order that does not follow from anything, and a man in the line for whom
# the ordinary supply of fear is simply not on issue.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The order nobody can explain. His mind is not his own, and the side is losing.
#
# `adapted`/`down`, THE RAREST PAIR IN THE LEDGER AND EXACTLY THIS. `adapted`
# is the mod's word for a commander who READ the field and changed what he was
# doing about it - and the whole of this moment is that he has changed
# something, on the basis of a reading nobody else in the host can reconstruct,
# while the numbers go the wrong way. The verb table hands out nothing for it
# in either direction, which is the right consequence: this is not an army
# being hurt or bracing or chasing, it is an army being sent somewhere.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads a trait and the
# side row and never asks about a rung, so the grant is live.
#
# TEN STRESS. Being obeyed while not being understood is its own weight, and
# the mod has one currency for weight.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT STATE THE ORDER, may not have anybody refuse it, and may not
# say whether it was right. Nobody in this toast knows that, including him. See
# the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_order_nobody_can_explain = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_his_mind_is_not_his_own_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man already at the second breaking point has less of himself left
		# to hold the rest of it down with.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			stress_level >= 2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = arbitrary
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_order_nobody_can_explain"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_order_nobody_can_explain_toast
			desc = bc_the_order_nobody_can_explain_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH SEVENTY-THREE. What the ground underfoot is worth.
#
# THE MOD HAS EIGHT QUESTIONS ABOUT THE FIELD AND ALL EIGHT ARE ABOUT ITS
# SHAPE. Six terrain bands, a river and a coast: what the place does to a line
# of men, where a horse can go, what hides an ambush. Not one of them asks what
# the place IS. A fat county with a market cross in it and a thin one with
# four cottages and a sheep track present identically to every gate in the
# mod, and the chronicle has therefore never once noticed the difference
# between fighting somewhere that matters and fighting somewhere that does not.
#
# BOTH MOMENTS ARE `adapted` OR `measured`, WHICH IS THE POINT AND NOT AN
# ACCIDENT. The ledger's own note on the verb table says those two KINDs are
# "how the commander READ the battle rather than what the battle DID to the
# army", and this whole axis is a reading. Nothing about a county's development
# level bleeds anybody, breaks anybody's horses or forms anybody's line, so
# neither moment hands out an army modifier and both fall through the table by
# design. They are also the two rarest pairs in the ledger - `measured`/`up` at
# seven sites and `adapted`/`down` at six - which is a second, smaller reason
# to be glad they fit.
#
# THE TWO GATES ARE DISJOINT BY CONSTRUCTION. At or below five, and at or above
# twenty; nothing is both, and the nine hundred and thirty-six counties in the
# middle get neither, so this axis stays quiet on most of the map instead of
# firing everywhere the way a single median split would.
#
# THE SIDE ROWS ARE THE UNOBVIOUS WAY ROUND, DELIBERATELY. The poor county goes
# to the man who is WINNING and the rich one to the knight who is LOSING. The
# obvious pairing - rich country you are winning, poor country you are losing -
# is two moments in which the ground agrees with the scoreboard and therefore
# says nothing the scoreboard was not already saying. Crossed, each one is a
# fact working against the other: a victory being won on ground that cannot be
# kept, and a defeat being given away across ground that was worth keeping.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The thin country. The county is poor, and the side is winning.
#
# `measured`/`up`, AND THE FALL-THROUGH IS THE CORRECT CONSEQUENCE. He is
# winning and he is going to keep winning, and the reading he has just done is
# that there is no reason to stop here - no walls to put men behind, no
# granary to sit on, no road junction that owning changes anything about. That
# is a judgement, not an event, and the verb table has nothing that means "he
# judged it well", which is exactly why `measured` exists.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the county, the
# opener and the side row, and never asks about a rung, so the grant is live.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE. Not for the win - the win is elsewhere - but for the host
# still moving at the end of an afternoon it could have spent standing on
# something.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_short_war AND NOT bc_the_chest_is_empty. Those two read
# `gold < 0`: they are about HIS purse and what being broke does to a man's
# choices. This one never looks at his purse and would fire identically for a
# man with a full treasury. The poverty is the ground's.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A HOLDING OR A VILLAGE, may not burn or take anything,
# and may not have him say the smallfolk here are worthless. He is assessing a
# position, not a people. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_thin_country = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_field_is_poor_country_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# With the numbers on his side as well, the decision to keep moving
		# costs him nothing and he makes it faster.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_side_is_outnumbering_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = impatient
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = greedy
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_thin_country"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_thin_country_toast
			desc = bc_the_thin_country_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH SEVENTY-FOUR. How the other man's afternoon is going.
#
# THE MOD ASKS NINE QUESTIONS ABOUT THE MAN ACROSS THE FIELD AND EVERY ONE OF
# THEM WOULD HAVE THE SAME ANSWER AT DAWN. Who he is - kin, countryman,
# heathen, of the house, a nemesis. What he is - a king, a better soldier, a
# worse one, present or absent. All of it was already true when the two hosts
# were still forming up. The chronicle has never once asked the only question
# about him that this particular afternoon can answer, which is whether it is
# going badly for him too.
#
# IT HAS KNOWN THE ANSWER THE WHOLE TIME. The rungs are character modifiers and
# these templates run for both sides of a combat, so the man opposite has been
# accumulating the mod's own marks all day. Reading them across the field is
# the chronicle looking at its own handwriting from the wrong end, and it is
# the first time anything in the mod has done that.
#
# BOTH MOMENTS TAKE THE OTHER MAN'S TROUBLE AS A FACT AND DIFFER ON WHAT IT IS
# WORTH. To the commander who is losing anyway it is worth nothing, and that is
# the more interesting half: it removes the last comfortable explanation for
# the state of his own line. To the knight in the front rank of a winning side
# it is worth a great deal, because it arrives as a change in what the men in
# front of him are doing, and he is close enough to use it before anybody with
# a horse and a view has finished writing it down.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The other man's trouble. The enemy commander is faltering and he is losing.
#
# `adapted`/`down`. He has spent some part of the last hour explaining the day
# to himself in terms of the man opposite - that it is going this way because
# that one is doing something well - and the news from the far bank is that the
# other one is not doing anything well either. So the explanation goes, and
# something has to be done on the basis of a field that is worse than he
# thought rather than a rival who is better than he thought. That is a reading
# and a change made on it, which is what `adapted` means, and it happens while
# the numbers go the wrong way. The verb table gives nothing for it: no army is
# bled, braced or launched by a man revising his opinion.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the ENEMY
# commander's rung and the side row and never asks about his own, so the grant
# is live and there is no self-reference here to worry about.
#
# TEN STRESS. Losing is one weight. Losing to a man who is also losing is
# another, and the mod has one currency for both.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_other_man_is_better. That template reads a martial score -
# a fact about the enemy that was true this morning and will be true at his
# next battle. This one is true only today. The two can both be valid on the
# same roll and they are saying opposite things when they are, which is
# correct: a better commander can still be having a worse afternoon.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT TELL HIM WHAT WENT WRONG OVER THERE and may not turn the
# battle round. He is looking at a banner and a pattern of runners, not at a
# report. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_other_mans_trouble = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_foe_commander_is_faltering_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who reads a field well for a living is the man most likely to
		# have been reading this one wrong all afternoon.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_other_mans_trouble"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_other_mans_trouble_toast
			desc = bc_the_other_mans_trouble_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH SEVENTY-FIVE. The men opposite were not trained on the same field.
#
# THE SETTING HAS TWO CONTINENTS AND THE MOD HAS NOTICED ONE. Ninety-two
# commander gates and sixty knight gates, and the only two that have ever asked
# where a man learned his trade are bc_foe_is_countryman_trigger, which asks
# whether two culture keys match, and bc_he_is_free_folk_trigger, which asks
# after one specific culture north of the Wall. Everything else in the mod is
# written as though every host on the map drills the same way, which is a
# strange thing to assume in a setting where half the interesting armies are
# horse archers, unarmoured spearmen or men with a curved sword and no shield
# at all.
#
# THE GATE IS ABOUT DRILL AND NOT ABOUT VIRTUE. Both toasts are written from
# inside a man who has trained for one kind of fight and is being given
# another. Neither is allowed to find the men opposite savage, cowardly, less
# than men, or in any way worse - being unfamiliar is the entire content of the
# observation, and a chronicle that let it become contempt would be a different
# and much cheaper mod.
#
# BOTH MOMENTS ARE ABOUT THE FIRST HOUR OF LEARNING SOMETHING. The commander
# does it badly, under pressure, by falling back on the one shape he trusts.
# The knight does it well, in the space of about four exchanges, because his
# scale of problem is small enough to solve. That is the same event at two
# magnifications, and the mod has room for both because the two pools roll
# separately.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The wrong war entirely. The foe is from across the sea and he is losing.
#
# `entrenched`/`down` AND bc_shieldwall, WHICH IS THE HONEST CONSEQUENCE. He
# does not have an answer to what is being done to him and he is not going to
# invent one in the next quarter of an hour, so he does the thing every
# commander of his training does when the field stops making sense: he makes
# the line thicker and shorter and tells it to stand. That is a real defensive
# benefit and it is also an admission, which is exactly what `entrenched` on a
# `down` swing has always meant in this ledger.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the enemy
# commander's culture and the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_lines_bogged_down OR bc_the_broken_ground_holds. Both of those
# are the GROUND refusing to cooperate, and both would fire in exactly the same
# way against an army that drilled the way his does. Here the ground is fine.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A PLACE, A PEOPLE OR A WEAPON. The gate knows only that
# the man opposite is outside the descent tree of the continent, which covers
# everything from a Braavosi captain to a khalasar, and a toast that named any
# of them would be wrong most of the time it fired. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_wrong_war_entirely = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_foe_is_from_across_the_sea_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The more of his trade he has, the more of it is currently the wrong
		# trade, and the further he has to come to admit it.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = strategist
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_wrong_war_entirely"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_wrong_war_entirely_toast
			desc = bc_the_wrong_war_entirely_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH SEVENTY-SIX. The season, which is not the terrain.
#
# THE MOD HAD A WINTER AND IT WAS THE WRONG ONE. bc_field_is_winter_trigger is
# frozen flats and glacier - a terrain band, a permanent property of a handful
# of provinces, as true in the eighth year of a long summer as in the third
# year of a winter. In a setting whose motto is about a season arriving, ninety
# -three commander gates and sixty-one knight gates had never asked whether it
# had.
#
# THE ARGUMENT IS TIME AND NOT COLD, WHICH IS THE WHOLE REASON THIS IS NOT
# bc_winter_bites. That template and bc_knight_stands_in_the_snow between them
# own everything the mod has to say about cold as a CONDITION - it hurts the
# army, and a hard man endures it. Neither of these two is about being cold.
# They are about a battle turning out to be a small event inside a much longer
# one, which is a thing only a real season can do to a day.
#
# THE SIDE ROWS ARE CROSSED FOR THE SAME REASON AS BATCH SEVENTY-THREE'S. The
# winning commander gets the news that his win is not the size of the problem;
# the losing knight gets the news that the problem has a deadline. Neither of
# those observations is available to a man whose day is going the other way.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The winter outlasts the war. It is winter, and the side is winning.
#
# `measured`/`up`. He has the field and is going to keep it, and the reading he
# has just done is about scale rather than about this afternoon: everything won
# here has to be fed through a season nobody can put an end date on. That is a
# judgement and not an event, and the verb table has nothing for a man
# correctly estimating a problem, which is what `measured` is for.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED, and granting it is not in tension
# with the sober reading. The host is winning and keeps winning; what has
# changed is only what he thinks winning is worth by spring.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE. For the win, which is real, and which the toast is careful
# not to take away from him.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_long_war. That reads how long THIS WAR has run - a number
# the mod keeps about a conflict - and would fire in the third summer of a
# border squabble. This reads the world.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT PUT A LENGTH ON THE WINTER. Nobody in this setting knows
# one, including the maesters, and a toast that guessed would be inventing the
# single fact the whole moment depends on being unavailable. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_winter_outlasts_the_war = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_season_is_winter_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who has to feed his own people through it is doing this
		# arithmetic already and does not need the battle to prompt him.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			is_landed = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = impatient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_winter_outlasts_the_war"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_winter_outlasts_the_war_toast
			desc = bc_the_winter_outlasts_the_war_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH SEVENTY-SEVEN. This is not the only war he is in.
#
# FIVE WAR QUESTIONS AND ALL FIVE ARE ABOUT THIS ONE. How long it has run, what
# kind it is, whose it is, whose it is not. Every one of them assumes a war,
# singular, with this man inside it and nothing else. The setting's defining
# conflict is named for having five of them going at once.
#
# THE TWO MOMENTS ARE THE SAME FACT SEEN FROM TWO HEIGHTS AND THEY DISAGREE
# ABOUT IT, WHICH IS THE POINT. The commander knows exactly how many men are
# committed elsewhere, because he committed them, and the knowledge is what
# stops him spending what would win this. The knight cannot see the other war
# at all and never will; what reaches him is only that the man on his left is
# not the man who was on his left last year, and that this one is managing.
#
# THE KNIGHT HALF WALKS TO HIS LORD, which is the only way a knight is ever in
# this moment - `any_character_war` lists a man's own wars and a sworn sword
# has none. The walk is the same one bc_knight_where_the_wages_are makes, with
# the same standing guard.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The other front. He has more than one war, and this one is going badly.
#
# `measured`/`down`. There is a decision available that would probably retrieve
# this afternoon and he is not going to take it, because the thing it would
# spend is already promised somewhere he cannot see from here. That is a
# judgement made against his own immediate interest, which is what `measured`
# has always been for, and it goes on the `down` swing because the afternoon is
# what it costs. The verb table gives nothing: no army is bled or braced by a
# man declining to commit it.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads his war list and
# the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS. Being right about this is worse than being wrong would have been,
# and the mod has one currency for that.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_other_half_of_the_realm OR bc_his_own_war. The first is
# gated on the war being a rebellion and is about which half of a realm he is
# standing in. The second asks whether this war is his. Both are about THIS
# war's nature. This one is about there being a second one, and it would fire
# identically on two ordinary border squabbles.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE OTHER WAR, its enemy, its front or its distance.
# The gate knows a count and nothing else. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_other_front = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_he_has_another_war_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The better he is at this, the more clearly he can see the move he is
		# refusing to make.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = reckless
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_other_front"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_other_front_toast
			desc = bc_the_other_front_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH SEVENTY-EIGHT. What is behind each of them if he does not get up.
#
# THE MOD WOUNDS MEN AND BURIES THEM AND HAS NEVER ASKED WHAT IT COSTS. Plan
# 6's gate puts a commander on his back, the aftermath events put him in the
# ground, and across ninety-five commander gates and sixty-three knight gates
# nothing has ever looked at who would be standing in his place a month later.
# Succession is the largest single subject the engine has and the chronicle has
# been fighting in front of it with its back turned.
#
# THE TWO MEN ARE AT OPPOSITE ENDS OF THE SAME FACT AND NEITHER GATE CONTAINS
# THE OTHER. One has something to hand on and the person it would go to cannot
# yet hold a sword. The other has nothing yet and is standing in a rank on the
# strength of what he is owed. A childless lord whose heir is a nephew answers
# yes to the first and no to the second; an eldest son with no land of his own
# answers no to the first and yes to the second.
#
# NEITHER MOMENT IS ABOUT FEAR OF DYING. The mod has that already, several
# times, in bc_he_looks_for_the_road and everything gated on craven. These two
# are about the arithmetic that survives a man - which is a different thing and
# is available to men who are not frightened at all.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The heir is a child. His successor is not an adult, and he is losing.
#
# `adapted`/`down`. Somewhere in the last half hour he has done a sum about
# what his own body is currently worth to people who are not on this field, and
# he has changed his conduct on the answer. He is not further back than he was
# because he is afraid; he is further back because the thing he is carrying is
# not his to spend. That is a reading of the situation and a change made on it,
# which is what `adapted` means, and the verb table hands out nothing, which is
# right - an army is not braced or bled by its commander revising his own
# price.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads his heir and the
# side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS. Knowing the sum is correct is not the same as being able to stand
# in the middle of it.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_green_boy, which reads `age <= 21` on him. That is a young
# man leading. This is a man old enough to have a child heir, and the two could
# fire on the same battle from opposite ends of it.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME OR AGE THE CHILD, may not show him, and may not have
# anybody on this field mention him. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_heir_is_a_child = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_his_heir_is_a_child_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The more there is to hand on, the louder the sum is.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = family_first
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = reckless
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_heir_is_a_child"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_heir_is_a_child_toast
			desc = bc_the_heir_is_a_child_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH SEVENTY-NINE. The body he was dealt, and what was decided about it.
#
# THE MOD READS WHAT A MAN CAN DO AND HAS NEVER READ WHAT HE WAS BORN WITH.
# `prowess` and `martial` sit on nearly every chance block in this file and
# both describe a man today. Nothing in ninety-six commander gates or
# sixty-four knight gates has ever asked what he has been carrying since birth
# and what the people around him settled about him before he could hold
# anything.
#
# ONE TRIGGER, TWO OPPOSITE SIDE ROWS. bc_his_body_was_dealt_to_him_trigger is
# the engine's own `num_of_bad_genetic_traits >= 1`, which is deliberately
# indiscriminate - a clubfoot and a stutter count the same. That is the point:
# neither moment is about the defect, both are about the verdict that came with
# it. A second gate at `>= 2` would be a strict subset and dead code, which is
# the same reason batch seventy-six used one winter trigger and not two bands.
#
# NEITHER MOMENT IS bc_he_is_unwell, which reads the illness flag - a thing
# that arrived and may leave. This was there first and will outlast the war.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The story is already written. He was born with something the world decided
# about, and he is losing.
#
# `entrenched`/`down`. He knows exactly what will be said about this afternoon
# before the afternoon is over, and he knows it will be said about his body and
# not about the ford or the numbers or the rain. The one thing that unwrites it
# is a line that is still standing at dusk, so that is what he spends the rest
# of the day producing - not a counterattack, not a gamble, a held shape while
# going backwards. The verb table hands out bc_shieldwall, which is right: an
# army braced while losing is exactly what this is.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads his birth and the
# side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_he_is_unwell. That template is about a man carrying a fever
# into a battle. This one is about a man carrying a verdict, and the two sets
# do not overlap - a man can be in both at once without either being the other.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME WHAT HE WAS BORN WITH. The gate covers a stutter and a
# clubfoot equally and the toast has to be true of both, so it may not describe
# his body, may not have him move badly, and may not have anybody look at him.
# See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_story_is_already_written = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_his_body_was_dealt_to_him_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Holding a shape on purpose, while losing, is a thing a man has to be
		# able to do as well as want to do.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_story_is_already_written"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_story_is_already_written_toast
			desc = bc_the_story_is_already_written_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH EIGHTY. What his own faith makes of him.
#
# FAITH IS READ FORTY-THREE TIMES IN THIS MOD AND EVERY ONE OF THEM IS ABOUT
# THE MAN OPPOSITE. bc_foe_is_heathen_trigger asks what the far side prays to;
# the eve and aftermath events ask what a victory or a burial is worth to a
# faith. Not one has ever turned the question round and asked what a man's own
# faith has already concluded about him.
#
# TWO TRIGGERS AND NEITHER IS THE OTHER'S NEGATION. num_sinful_traits and
# num_virtuous_traits are two independent tallies over the same trait list, not
# two ends of one scale: a man with three sins and three virtues answers yes to
# both, and a mild man with no strong traits answers no to both. The two
# templates are therefore not mutually exclusive, and they roll in different
# pools in any case.
#
# THE THRESHOLD IS TWO ON BOTH SIDES. One sinful trait is most of the adult
# population under most faiths, and a gate that fires for nearly everybody is
# not a gate.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# His own faith has a settled opinion of him, and he is winning anyway.
#
# `measured`/`up`. Nothing on a field has ever asked what a man is. Ground,
# numbers, distance, the hour - that is the whole list, and he worked that out
# a long time before today and has been quietly good at it ever since, in a way
# that men who expect the world to keep accounts never quite manage. The verb
# table hands out nothing, which is right: this is a reading, and an army is
# not braced or bled by its commander having stopped expecting to be rewarded.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads his sins and the
# side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_foe_is_heathen OR ANY OF THE FAITH TEMPLATES, all of which are
# about the men opposite. This one never looks across the field at all.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A SIN, may not name his faith or its god, may not put a
# septon or priest of any kind on the page, may not have him be defiant about
# it, and may not have him win because of what he is. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_field_keeps_no_accounts = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_his_own_faith_counts_against_him_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Reading a field for what it is, and nothing else, is a skill before
		# it is a disposition.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = cynical
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = zealous
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_field_keeps_no_accounts"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_field_keeps_no_accounts_toast
			desc = bc_the_field_keeps_no_accounts_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH EIGHTY-ONE. What he was raised to be.
#
# `has_education_*` APPEARS ZERO TIMES IN THIS MOD. Ninety-eight commander
# gates and sixty-six knight gates read what a man can do and not one has ever
# read what somebody spent his childhood trying to make him into. Education is
# the single largest statement the engine makes about a character's intended
# shape and the chronicle has never opened it.
#
# BOTH TRIGGERS WRAP AGOT'S OWN, which are five-arm ORs rather than vanilla's
# four - a hand-written list here would miss every top-rank education in the
# game. The two are mutually exclusive by construction, since a character has
# exactly one education trait.
#
# NEITHER GATE IS "NOT RAISED FOR WAR". That negative fires for most of the
# peerage and would not be a gate at all. Naming the education a man did get is
# narrower and says more about him.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He was raised for books, and he is losing.
#
# `bloodied`/`down` AND bc_bloodied. He is doing the only thing he was ever
# taught to do with a difficult problem: taking it apart carefully, in order,
# checking each piece before moving to the next. It is a good method and it is
# slower than a battle, and the difference between the two speeds is being paid
# for in men. That is not a failure of nerve and it is not cowardice - it is a
# man applying the wrong instrument beautifully, and the army wearing the cost.
# The verb is bc_bloodied because the cost is exactly where the pair says it
# is: on the line, not on him.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads his education and
# the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_green_boy, WHICH READS `age <= 21`. That is a man who has
# not done this yet. This is a man who may have done it a dozen times and was
# still never built for it, and a grey-haired maester-taught lord answers no to
# the first and yes to this one.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A BOOK, A MAESTER, A LIBRARY OR A CHAIN, may not have
# him quote or remember anything he read, may not have a captain overrule him
# or save him, may not have anybody blame him, and may not have him blame
# himself. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_wrong_instrument_beautifully = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_he_was_raised_for_books_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The better he is at the thing he was taught, the sharper the mismatch
		# between it and the afternoon.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			learning >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = humble
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_wrong_instrument_beautifully"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_wrong_instrument_beautifully_toast
			desc = bc_the_wrong_instrument_beautifully_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH EIGHTY-TWO. How many banners are standing on this side.
#
# THE MOD HAS NEVER ASKED HOW MANY LORDS ARE ON ITS OWN SIDE. Every side-level
# question in the file treats the side as one undifferentiated quantity of men
# with one man at the top of it. A host in this setting is a stack of banners
# and the man the battle is named after is not the only man on the field
# giving orders.
#
# `any_side_commander` IS THE ENGINE'S OWN LIST OF "the commanders of every
# army on the side, not just the one leading the battle" - a count of
# independent hosts, which is to say a count of lords who each brought men and
# each expect to be consulted. Nothing here had ever read it.
#
# THREE AND ONE, WITH THE GAP LEFT EMPTY ON PURPOSE. Two banners is the
# ordinary case and neither moment is about the ordinary case.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Three banners or more, and every one of them did its part today.
#
# `ordered`/`up` AND bc_pursuit. What is unusual here is not that the men
# fought - men usually fight - but that four or five separate lords, none of
# whom is obliged to like any of the others, each did the thing they had agreed
# to do at roughly the hour they had agreed to do it. That is the rarest event
# on any field in this setting and it produces the one consequence a coalition
# almost never gets: an intact order to pursue with. The verb table hands out
# bc_pursuit, which is exactly what the pair is for.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the banner count and
# the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THE CHANCE ROW READS `diplomacy`, WHICH IS ALMOST UNIQUE IN THIS FILE. Every
# other commander template weights on martial or prowess. Holding four lords to
# one plan for one afternoon is not a martial skill and pretending it is would
# be the wrong reading of the moment.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_host_is_vast, which counts men. Three small banners answer yes
# to this and no to that, and one enormous host under one lord answers the
# reverse.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A HOUSE, A SIGIL OR A LORD, may not stage a council,
# may not have anybody thank or congratulate anybody, and may not have him take
# credit for it. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_every_banner_did_its_part = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_the_host_is_many_banners_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Holding four lords to one plan is not a martial skill.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			diplomacy >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = arbitrary
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_every_banner_did_its_part"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_every_banner_did_its_part_toast
			desc = bc_every_banner_did_its_part_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH EIGHTY-THREE. The sword itself.
#
# ARTIFACTS APPEAR NOWHERE IN THIS MOD. A hundred commander gates and
# sixty-eight knight gates read men, ground, weather, numbers, wars, faiths and
# births, and not one has ever asked what is in anybody's hand. In this setting
# there are a few hundred swords worth more than the men carrying them and
# every one of them has a name, and this chronicle has fought two hundred
# moments without noticing a single one.
#
# BOTH TRIGGERS WRAP AGOT'S `agot_is_valyrian_steel_artifact`, which is read on
# an ARTIFACT rather than a character, and both use
# `any_equipped_character_artifact` rather than `any_character_artifact` -
# a named sword in a vault at home is not on this field.
#
# THE TWO ARE THE SAME FACT FROM ITS TWO ENDS AND BOTH CAN BE TRUE AT ONCE,
# which in this setting is a perfectly ordinary afternoon.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# There is one on the far side, and he is losing.
#
# `measured`/`down`. He has spent the last hour running his whole afternoon
# around the movements of a single man, because that is what a blade like that
# forces a commander to do - it does not win a battle by itself and it makes
# every arrangement in front of it temporary. So the orders go where it is not,
# and the line thins where it is, and the arithmetic never quite closes. That
# is a reading of the field and a losing one, and the verb table hands out
# nothing, which is right: an army is not braced or bled by its commander
# tracking one man.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the far side's
# equipment and the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_foe_is_formidable, which reads the man. A middling knight can
# be handed an heirloom by a dying grandfather and a great captain can carry
# ordinary steel his whole life, and the two gates disagree constantly.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE SWORD OR THE HOUSE, may not describe the blade -
# no ripples, no smoke, no colour - may not have anybody say what it is, may
# not have him consider sending men to take it, and may not kill him. See the
# loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_bright_thing_on_the_far_side = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_the_foe_carries_valyrian_steel_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Building an afternoon around where one man is standing is a
		# calculation before it is a fear.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = reckless
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_bright_thing_on_the_far_side"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_bright_thing_on_the_far_side_toast
			desc = bc_the_bright_thing_on_the_far_side_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH EIGHTY-FOUR. What has already been taken off him.
#
# THE MOD READS TWO KINDS OF BODY AND NEITHER IS THIS ONE. bc_he_is_unwell
# reads the illness flag - a thing that arrived and may leave. Batch
# seventy-nine reads the count of bad genetic traits - a thing that was there
# before he was. Between them sits the largest category of damaged men in this
# setting by a very long way: the ones who walked onto a field whole and did
# not walk off it that way, which in a mod entirely about fields is a strange
# thing to have missed for eighty-three batches.
#
# BOTH GATES READ THE TRAIT AND NOT AGOT'S APPEARANCE WRAPPERS. Those wrappers
# are questions about how a man is DRAWN - which patch his portrait wears -
# and a one-eyed lord whose portrait never got a patch flag is still a one-eyed
# lord. Neither trait carries the illness flag and neither is genetic, so these
# two are disjoint from the mod's existing body gates by construction.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He has half the field he used to have, and he is losing.
#
# `adapted`/`down`. Everything about how he reads a battle was rebuilt some
# years ago around a side he has to turn his whole head to look at, and the
# rebuild works - he has fought and won since. What it costs is a fraction of a
# second per look, and today is one of the afternoons where the fractions are
# adding up faster than he can spend them. That is a reading of the situation
# and a losing one; the verb table hands out nothing, which is right, because
# an army is not braced or bled by how quickly its commander's head turns.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads one trait and the
# side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_he_is_unwell OR bc_the_story_is_already_written. The first is
# a fever, the second is a thing he was born with. This one has a date, and he
# knows what it is.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT SAY HOW HE LOST IT, may not name the battle or the man, may
# not have him remember it, may not put a patch or a scar on the page, may not
# have anybody notice or accommodate him, and may not let the moment be about
# courage. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_fractions_are_adding_up = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_he_lost_an_eye_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The better he reads a field, the more of the afternoon is running
		# through the part of it that costs him time.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = lazy
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_fractions_are_adding_up"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_fractions_are_adding_up_toast
			desc = bc_the_fractions_are_adding_up_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}

# ===========================================================================
# BATCH EIGHTY-FIVE. Whose name this is.
#
# THE MOD HAS ASKED ABOUT HOUSES ONCE AND ONLY ACROSS THE LINE. Every house
# question in eighty-four batches is bc_foe_is_house_trigger, which is about
# the man opposite. Where he stands INSIDE the name he carries has never been
# read, and in this setting that is the fact that organises most of a man's
# life: a Frey is not a fact about a man, but being the Frey, or being one of
# the ninety who are not, is.
#
# TWO TEMPLATES AND THEY CANNOT BOTH BE TRUE. The second gate carries an
# explicit NOT on the first, so the head of a house never answers yes to it.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The name is his, and the name is winning.
#
# `measured`/`up` - the smallest cell in the whole census. Every account of
# this afternoon that gets written down anywhere will be written under one
# word, and the word is his. Not his family's, not his father's: the ledgers
# in six castles will say the house did this, and he is the man the word
# currently means. That is not courage and it is not a manoeuvre - it is a
# reading of what an afternoon is FOR, arriving on a day when the reading is
# a good one, and the verb table hands out nothing, which is right.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the house link and
# the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_field_keeps_no_accounts, which is a man deciding the
# afternoon will not be written down accurately by anybody. This one is about
# the fact that it will be written down under a particular word.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE HOUSE, may not quote a word or a sigil or a seat,
# may not have him think about an ancestor, may not have anybody address him
# by the name on the page, and may not let him be pleased with himself. See
# the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_word_they_will_write_it_under = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_the_name_is_his_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who reads a field well is a man who is already thinking past
		# the end of it.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_word_they_will_write_it_under"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_word_they_will_write_it_under_toast
			desc = bc_the_word_they_will_write_it_under_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH EIGHTY-SIX. What his own world has already convicted him of.
#
# THE MOD READS WHAT MEN THINK OF HIM AND HAS NEVER READ WHAT HE DID. Dread
# is a feeling in other people that decays if he stops feeding it, and it is
# the only thing of its kind this chronicle has ever asked about. Tyranny is
# the sum of what he has taken without a right to it, and the kinslayer traits
# are the one crime this entire setting agrees about across every faith in it.
# Neither had ever been read.
#
# THE TWO GATES ARE NOT DISJOINT AND DO NOT NEED TO BE. They measure different
# things - a running total against a lord, and a fact about a man - and they
# sit in different pools, which roll separately.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# What is holding this army together is not affection, and the side is losing.
#
# `entrenched`/`down` AND bc_shieldwall. There are two things that keep men
# standing in a line that is going backwards and he made his choice between
# them a long time ago and has been paying for it ever since in coin he does
# not think of as coin. The line holds. It holds for the reason he built it to
# hold, which is not a reason anybody will put in a song, and he would rather
# it were the other one and it is far too late in the afternoon and in his life
# for that.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads a running total and
# the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_they_fear_me_more, which reads `dread >= medium_dread`.
# Dread is what his men feel; this is what he has actually done, and the two
# come apart constantly - a mild lord can be dreaded by rumour and a monster
# can be perfectly comfortable in every hall he owns.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A CRIME, may not name a victim, may not have him
# justify anything, may not have anybody obey him on the page out of fear, and
# may not have him be sorry. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_other_reason_men_stand = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_his_own_people_have_a_ledger_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who is good at this has been good at it for years, and the
		# ledger is what years of it look like.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = callous
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_other_reason_men_stand"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_other_reason_men_stand_toast
			desc = bc_the_other_reason_men_stand_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH EIGHTY-SEVEN. The crown that is not on him yet.
#
# AGOT SHIPS A CORONATION SYSTEM AND THIS MOD HAD NEVER LOOKED AT IT. The gap
# between being proclaimed and being crowned is measured in this setting in
# years and wars, and eighty-six batches read who a man is, what he believes,
# who he is related to and what he has taken without ever reading whether the
# thing he calls himself has been made official by anybody.
#
# BOTH TEMPLATES ARE `adapted`, WHICH IS DELIBERATE. The ledger keeps that KIND
# for how a man READ the battle rather than what the battle did to the army,
# and a crown is not a fact about a field - it is a fact about what an
# afternoon is going to be understood to have meant. Neither template hands out
# a modifier and neither should.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The ceremony is downstream of this, and this is going badly.
#
# `adapted`/`down` - the smallest cell in the census. There is no oil and no
# septon and no crowd behind him; there is a claim, which is a sentence people
# say, and the only thing in the world that turns that sentence into a fact is
# a run of afternoons like this one going the right way. Today is not going the
# right way. He is not being beaten out of a kingdom he has - he is being
# beaten out of the argument that he has one, which is a different and thinner
# thing to be standing on at the fourth hour, and he can feel exactly how thin
# it is.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads two AGOT triggers
# and the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_story_is_already_written or any of the succession
# templates. Those are about what he was handed. This is about a thing that has
# not happened yet and may not, and about the fact that nothing except days
# like this one can make it happen.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A CROWN, A SEPT, A SEPTON OR A THRONE, may not have him
# imagine the ceremony, may not have anybody call him king or refuse to, may
# not name a rival claimant, and may not have him doubt his right. See the loc
# header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_argument_that_he_has_one = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_the_crown_is_not_on_him_yet_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who reads a field well is a man who can see precisely how much
		# of his claim is being spent this hour.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_argument_that_he_has_one"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_argument_that_he_has_one_toast
			desc = bc_the_argument_that_he_has_one_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
