# ===========================================================================
# BATCH EIGHTY-EIGHT. The marriage.
#
# `is_married`, `any_spouse` AND `num_of_spouses` ALL RETURNED ZERO OVER THE
# WHOLE MOD. Eighty-seven batches read a man's blood, his house, his heirs, his
# education, his faith, his body and his crimes, and never once read the single
# arrangement that most of those things exist to produce. In a setting where a
# wedding is a treaty, that is the largest thing this chronicle had not
# noticed.
#
# NEITHER GATE IS `is_married = yes`, which is nearly every landed man over
# twenty in the game. One asks whether the marriage and the battle are the same
# object; the other asks whether there is a child on the way.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man on the other side is her brother, or her father, and this is going
# badly.
#
# `ordered`/`down` AND NO MODIFIER. He is losing and he is losing carefully,
# and the care is not tactical. There is a version of this afternoon that ends
# with a field full of her family and a version that ends with a field full of
# his, and the second one is arriving; what he can still control is how much of
# the first one goes with it. So the line comes back in order and it comes back
# slowly and nothing gets committed that cannot be recalled, because whatever
# else tonight is, there is a room somewhere in it that he will have to sit
# down in.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads a spouse iteration
# and the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_foe_is_house_trigger or bc_foe_is_kin_trigger. Those are men
# who share his
# name or his ancestors, and the mod has read them for a long time. This one
# is a stranger who became family by contract, which in this setting is the
# more binding of the two and the one that is easier to be standing opposite.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME HER, may not say what she is to the man opposite, may
# not have him picture her, may not mention a wedding or a contract or an
# alliance, may not have him regret the match, and may not have him think about
# what she will hear. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_a_room_somewhere_in_tonight = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_he_married_into_the_other_side_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Coming back in order while losing is the hardest thing a commander
		# does and the one that most rewards being good at this.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = just
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = arbitrary
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_a_room_somewhere_in_tonight"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_a_room_somewhere_in_tonight_toast
			desc = bc_a_room_somewhere_in_tonight_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH EIGHTY-NINE. The paper that put them here.
#
# `is_allied_to` AND `is_allied_in_war` BOTH RETURNED ZERO OVER THE WHOLE MOD.
# Eighty-eight batches read blood, house, faith, tongue, education, body,
# crime, crown and marriage - every way one man can be bound to another except
# the one that is written down and signed. Half the hosts in this setting are
# two or three realms standing next to each other because of a document, and
# nothing here had ever asked whether the man forty yards away is here for the
# same reason.
#
# ONE GATE READS THE ARRANGEMENT FROM THE TOP AND ONE FROM THE BOTTOM. They can
# both be true on the same field at the same time on different men, which is
# the correct behaviour: an alliance looks like a colleague from one end and
# like a stranger's document from the other.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The seam between two hosts, and this is going badly.
#
# `bloodied`/`down` AND bc_bloodied, and the modifier is doing real work rather
# than decorating a mood. Two armies standing beside each other are two armies
# with a join in them, and a join is a place. It is not that the allied men are
# bad or unwilling; it is that no amount of willingness makes one line out of
# two, and on a bad afternoon the far side finds the place where the orders
# stop coming from the same mouth and puts everything it has into it. That is
# where the cost is being paid this hour, and the army wears it.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads a side iteration
# and the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_every_banner_did_its_part, which is many banners doing the
# thing they were asked to do on a good day. Every banner here is doing exactly
# what it was asked. The problem is not obedience and never was.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE ALLY OR HIS REALM, may not blame him, may not have
# him fail at anything, may not have anybody argue about precedence or orders,
# and may not have the alliance break on the page. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_join_is_a_place = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_an_ally_stands_on_this_side_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Seeing where a two-headed line is going to open, and seeing it in
		# time, is the whole of what being good at this means.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = trusting
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_join_is_a_place"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_the_join_is_a_place_toast
			desc = bc_the_join_is_a_place_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}

# ===========================================================================
# BATCH NINETY. Owned and held are two different questions.
#
# `county_controller` RETURNED ZERO OVER THE WHOLE MOD.
# bc_the_ground_is_his_own_trigger walks location -> county -> holder and asks
# who this county BELONGS to, which is a question about a document in a room
# somewhere. The engine also tracks who is standing on it with soldiers, which
# during a war is very often somebody else, and which is the only one of the
# two questions that affects where a man can get water this afternoon.
# Eighty-nine batches read the deed and never once read the occupation.
#
# THE FIRST GATE IS DISJOINT FROM THE OLDER OWNERSHIP TRIGGER BY CONSTRUCTION.
# Its holder row is the exact negation of that trigger's holder row, so no man
# on any field can answer yes to both.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He took this county eleven days ago, and today is going badly.
#
# `adapted`/`down` - the smallest cell in the census. Everything he knows about
# this ground he has learned since the middle of the month, most of it from
# people who would rather he drowned in the river he is asking about. There is
# a ford somewhere upstream that would have been worth knowing about this
# morning and he does not know about it, and the man on the other side has
# known about it since he was nine. That is not a failure of nerve or of
# planning; it is a reading of a field built out of eleven days of hearsay,
# arriving on the afternoon when the difference gets charged for.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads two title links and
# the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_ground_is_his_own OR ANY OF THE bc_field_* TEMPLATES. The
# first is a man on his own land, and the two gates are exact negations of each
# other on the holder row. The field templates ask what the ground is MADE of -
# forest, marsh, snow - which is a question the ground answers the same way for
# both armies. This one is about who grew up being told where the water is.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE COUNTY OR ANY PLACE IN IT, may not have him regret
# taking it, may not mention a siege or a garrison or a burning, may not have a
# local man refuse him anything on the page, and may not have him blame a
# scout. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_eleven_days_of_hearsay = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_the_ground_is_held_not_owned_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The better he is at reading ground, the more precisely he can feel
		# the part of it he was never told.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = lazy
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_eleven_days_of_hearsay"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_eleven_days_of_hearsay_toast
			desc = bc_eleven_days_of_hearsay_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH NINETY-ONE. What was chosen for him, and what he chose.
#
# `has_lifestyle` RETURNED ZERO OVER THE WHOLE MOD. Batch eighty-one read
# education, which is a decision taken about a boy of eight by adults with
# their own reasons and closed for good by sixteen. A lifestyle is the other
# half of the same man: what he does with his own hours once the hours are his,
# and the engine tracks it separately for exactly that reason.
#
# NEITHER OF THIS BATCH'S TWO GATES READS A LIFESTYLE ON ITS OWN. A bare
# `has_lifestyle = martial_lifestyle` on a commander would fire for a very
# large share of the men who command anything, and a gate that fires for nearly
# everybody is not a gate. What is worth reading is the DISAGREEMENT between
# the two fields: the boy who was pointed at war and walked somewhere else, and
# the boy who was pointed anywhere else and walked back.
#
# THE PAIR IS THE MIRROR OF BATCH EIGHTY-ONE'S PAIR. That batch put the man
# raised for books on the losing side and the man raised for the dark on the
# winning one, and both of its moments were about an upbringing meeting a
# field. These two are about an upbringing being OVERRULED by twenty years of
# a man's own afternoons, which is a different sentence about the same two
# facts, and each of these gates is disjoint from the eighty-one gate that sits
# on the same row of the census.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He was raised to do this and does something else, and the something else is
# what is working.
#
# `adapted`/`up`. Everything he is doing well this afternoon he learned
# somewhere that was not a training yard: keeping the count, holding a thing
# back that he has not been asked for yet, moving the pieces in the order that
# does not need them moved twice. The martial education is in him somewhere and
# has been for thirty years, and it is not what he is reaching for. He is
# running a battle the way he runs everything else, and today that is turning
# out to have been the right instrument all along. That is a reading of the
# field and a change made on it, which is what the KIND means, and the verb
# table hands out no modifier, which is right: an army is not braced or bled
# because the man at the top of it is using his own habits.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads an education, a
# lifestyle and the side row, and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_wrong_instrument_beautifully. That template requires a
# LEARNING education and this one requires a MARTIAL one, so the two gates
# cannot both be true of any man; that one is on the losing side and is
# `bloodied`/`down`; and its argument is a scholar discovering that his
# training does not apply here. This one is a soldier's son discovering that
# the training he did not take applies better than the one he did.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_taught_to_do_this, AND NEITHER GATE CONTAINS THE OTHER. That
# template reads `education_martial_4` alone and is `ordered`/`up`: the best
# possible training doing exactly what it was meant to do. This one takes a
# martial education of ANY grade - the question is what the boy was pointed at,
# not how well it took - and then adds a row that template never had, the
# absence of the lifestyle. A martial_4 who spends his life in the yard answers
# yes there and no here; a martial_1 who spends his life on his accounts
# answers no there and yes here; and the man who is both is simply a man two
# different moments can find, on different afternoons, for different reasons.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A LIFESTYLE, a focus, a perk or a tree, may not have
# him think about his tutor, may not have him name the thing he does instead,
# may not have anybody praise him for it on the page, and may not let him be
# pleased with himself. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_habits_he_actually_kept = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_he_was_pointed_at_war_and_left_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The education did take, whatever he did with the rest of his life,
		# and a man who kept the skill notices sooner which half of himself
		# is doing the work.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = lazy
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_habits_he_actually_kept"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_habits_he_actually_kept_toast
			desc = bc_the_habits_he_actually_kept_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH NINETY-TWO. The household, as opposed to the levy.
#
# Every court-position keyword returned zero over the whole mod. Ninety-one
# batches read blood, oath, land, faith, tongue, crime, upbringing and
# wages-by-the-company, and never once read the plainest arrangement a hall
# contains: a man on somebody's payroll who lives in his rooms. It is not
# vassalage, not kinship and not a mercenary contract - it is employment, it is
# renewed rather than inherited, and it puts a particular kind of man in a
# line.
#
# THE PAIR IS THE SAME ARRANGEMENT FROM BOTH ENDS, which is the shape batches
# eighty-five and eighty-seven used, and the two pools roll separately so they
# never compete for an afternoon.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The men holding it are the ones he pays.
#
# `measured`/`up`. It is going well, and the reason it is going well is
# standing forty yards away with a face he has seen at breakfast for nine
# years. That produces an accounting rather than a feeling: he knows what each
# of those men is owed, when he last raised it, which of them has people in his
# kitchens, and what it will cost him - in money and in the very short list of
# competent men who will take the work - if this afternoon uses them up. He
# spends them anyway, because they are the ones who will do it. That is a
# reading of the field rather than a thing the field did to the army, which is
# what the KIND means, and the verb table hands out no modifier, which is
# right: nobody is braced or bled by the commander doing a sum.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the knight list and
# the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_his_blood_in_the_ranks, THE OTHER MOMENT BUILT ON
# bc_side_has_kin_knight_trigger. That gate is `is_close_family_of` - blood and
# marriage - and this one is a payroll. A hired huntsman is not close family of
# the man who hires him and a brother in the knight list is not on anybody's
# wages; the man who is both exists and is rare, and neither gate can be
# reached from the other by dropping a row.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_they_are_not_his_smallfolk OR bc_knight_where_the_wages_are,
# THE TWO FREE-COMPANY MOMENTS. A company is a contract with a body of men and
# ends when the contract does. A court position is one man, in his hall, renewed, and the
# whole weight of this moment is that the commander knows his name and what he
# eats.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME AN OFFICE - no champion, no huntsman, no bodyguard, no
# master-at-arms - may not put a number of coins or a sum of wages on the page,
# may not have him decide to reward anybody afterwards, may not have any of
# them speak to him, and may not make him fond of them. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_men_he_knows_the_names_of = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_a_man_off_his_own_payroll_is_here_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Knowing which forty yards of a line is doing the work is the
		# skill, and it is the same skill that puts a name to the men
		# standing in it.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = impatient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_men_he_knows_the_names_of"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_men_he_knows_the_names_of_toast
			desc = bc_the_men_he_knows_the_names_of_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH NINETY-THREE. The one he buried.
#
# `any_former_spouse` returned zero over the whole mod. Batch eighty-eight read
# the marriage a man is currently inside and stopped there; the engine also
# keeps the marriages that ended, and in Westeros the overwhelming majority of
# them ended the one way. `is_alive = no` keeps the iterator to the burials,
# and `is_married` - which batch eighty-eight rejected as a gate on its own,
# because it is nearly every landed man in the game - does real work beside a
# burial, because it is the only question anybody actually asks about one.
#
# THE TWO GATES ARE DISJOINT BY CONSTRUCTION on the `is_married` row over an
# identical burial row.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# There is nobody left to be careful for, and he is winning.
#
# `wild`/`up`. Every other man with rank on this field carries a small
# permanent brake fitted somewhere behind the breastbone, and it is a face, and
# it is why he takes the third-best line instead of the best one and comes back
# in the evening to a hall where somebody asks how it went. That fitting was
# taken out of this man some years ago and never replaced. It does not make him
# brave - he would tell you flatly that it has nothing to do with courage - it
# simply means that when the far side opens this afternoon there is no quiet
# internal argument to have before he goes into the gap, and so he goes into it
# a half-hour earlier than a married man would. It works. It is also the
# reason the horse lines will be short tomorrow, which is what `wild` means and
# why it is not `ordered`: the rung and the spent horses come out of the same
# decision and arrive in the same breath.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED, AND bc_spent_horse COMES FROM THE
# VERB TABLE, NOT FROM HERE. The gate reads a marriage row and a burial row and
# the side row, and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_wrath_takes_him OR bc_the_old_grudge, THE TWO `wild`/`up`
# MOMENTS THE LEDGER COMMENT NAMES. Both of those are a man being carried by something hot -
# a temper, a name he has been waiting years to meet. This one is the absence
# of something cold. Nothing is driving him; a thing that used to hold him back
# is missing, and the arithmetic comes out the same way for a completely
# different reason.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_heir_is_a_child. That reads what his death costs the
# realm and makes him careful. This reads what his death costs one household,
# finds the household already gone, and makes him quick.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME HER, may not say how she died, may not say how long
# ago, may not have him picture her or remember a room, may not have him say or
# think that he wants to die, may not have anybody on the field know any of it,
# and may not make him tragic. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_nothing_waiting_at_the_other_end = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_he_buried_her_and_left_the_place_empty_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Seeing the gap at all is the skill. Going into it without the
		# argument first is what this moment is.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = brave
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_nothing_waiting_at_the_other_end"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_nothing_waiting_at_the_other_end_toast
			desc = bc_nothing_waiting_at_the_other_end_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH NINETY-FOUR. The paper that is still good.
#
# `has_truce` returned zero over the whole mod. Ninety-three batches read what
# two men are to each other by blood, faith, tongue, oath, land and wages, and
# never once read the one thing they may have actually signed. A truce is the
# only bond in the game that is a document, with a term and a year and a defeat
# behind it, and it does not stop either of them being called into somebody
# else's war on opposite sides.
#
# ONE TRIGGER SERVES BOTH POOLS because it never touches `root`. The two
# moments below take the same fact and do completely different things with it,
# and the pools roll separately, so they never compete for an afternoon.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He is winning a battle against a man he is at peace with.
#
# `measured`/`up`. There is a document in a chest at home with both their seals
# on it and a term on it that has years left to run, and nothing about today
# breaks it, because neither of them declared anything - a third man did, and
# called them from opposite ends of a list. The battle is real and the paper is
# also real, and the arithmetic that follows from holding both of those at once
# is what this moment is. It is going his way, so the arithmetic has teeth: he
# is the one who gets to decide how far this goes, and every yard past the
# necessary one is a yard he will be explaining across a table in a year, under
# the same seals, to the same man. So it stays a battle rather than becoming a
# settling. That is a reading rather than a thing the field did to the army,
# which is what the KIND means, and the verb table hands out no modifier, which
# is right: nobody's line is braced or bled by their commander remembering a
# document.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads a truce and the side
# row and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_old_grudge OR ANYTHING BUILT ON THE NEMESIS RELATION.
# Those are built on a history of blood between two men and are hot. A truce is the opposite kind of
# history: it is what got WRITTEN DOWN after the blood, it has a date on it, and
# the whole point of this moment is that the writing is still working.
#
# THIS IS NOT ANY OF THE BOND-LADDER MOMENTS - kin, house, countryman, faith.
# All of those read what two men ARE to each other, which nobody chose. This
# reads the one thing they agreed to.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT QUOTE THE DOCUMENT, may not name the war that produced it,
# may not say which of them lost it, may not have him consider breaking it, may
# not have a herald or a parley on the page, may not have the two of them see
# each other, and may not make him merciful in so many words. See the loc
# header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_seals_are_still_wet_enough = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_the_truce_still_holds_across_the_field_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Knowing exactly how far a won afternoon can be pushed is the same
		# skill as knowing how far it must be.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = calm
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = vengeful
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_seals_are_still_wet_enough"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_seals_are_still_wet_enough_toast
			desc = bc_the_seals_are_still_wet_enough_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH NINETY-FIVE. What the noise on the far side means.
#
# Every language keyword returned zero over the whole mod. Ninety-four batches
# read culture six ways and never once read the layer underneath it, which is
# the one that decides whether a man can UNDERSTAND the field he is standing
# on. AGOT ships twenty-six languages of its own, and the two axes genuinely
# cross: two Westerosi cultures that share nothing else share a tongue, and two
# men on the same side of the sea can still have no word in common.
#
# `knows_court_language_of` takes a character rather than a language key, so
# nothing here rots when AGOT adds a tongue, and court language is the right
# question for a battle: it is the language the orders around a man are shouted
# in.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The whole far side is weather, and it is going badly.
#
# `adapted`/`down`. A commander reads an enemy with his ears as much as his
# eyes and never thinks about it, because he has never been without it: the
# shouted correction that tells you a line is being dressed rather than
# reformed, the one name being called over and over that tells you who has just
# gone down, the moment the noise stops being orders and becomes men talking to
# each other. All of that is free and none of it is available today. What
# reaches him across the four hundred yards has exactly as much information in
# it as rain on a roof, and so he is running the afternoon on sight alone,
# which is half the instrument, on the day it is going wrong. That is a reading
# of the field rather than a thing the field did to the army, which is what the
# KIND means, and the verb table hands out no modifier, which is right: an army
# is not braced or bled because its commander cannot follow the shouting.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads a language and the
# side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_wrong_war_entirely OR ANY OTHER CULTURE TEMPLATE. Those
# read who the far side ARE. This reads whether anything they
# say arrives, and the two cross in both directions - a Reachman fighting a
# Northman is fighting a foreigner he can hear perfectly, and a man facing the
# Old Tongue has not crossed any water at all.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_eleven_days_of_hearsay, THE OTHER `adapted`/`down` MOMENT ON A
# MISSING FACT. That one is about ground he was told about too recently and the
# information is in principle obtainable, just not by dusk. This one is about a
# channel that is closed for the whole afternoon and would be closed on any
# ground in the world.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A LANGUAGE, may not have anybody translate, may not put
# an interpreter or a captured man on the page, may not have him mishear
# something and act on it, and may not have him find the far side barbarous.
# See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_it_arrives_as_weather = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_the_far_side_is_only_noise_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The better he is at reading a field, the more precisely he can
		# feel the half of the instrument that is missing.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_it_arrives_as_weather"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_it_arrives_as_weather_toast
			desc = bc_it_arrives_as_weather_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH NINETY-SIX. The fostering.
#
# `has_relation_guardian` and `has_relation_ward` returned zero over the whole
# mod. Ninety-five batches read blood, house, oath, wages, tongue, land and
# paper, and never once read the arrangement Westeros actually runs on: a boy
# sent to another man's hall for ten years to be made into something, kept by
# the engine as a named relation for as long as both men live.
#
# THE DIRECTION IS THE WHOLE REASON THERE ARE TWO GATES. They are the same fact
# from opposite ends, they are disjoint by construction because each carries
# the negation of the other, and the moments built on them have nothing in
# common except a hall thirty years ago.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He is beating a man he taught, and he knows the next thing that man will do.
#
# `ordered`/`up`. This is the only moment in the mod where the pursuit modifier
# is not a compliment. He is winning, the far side has started to give ground,
# and he knows precisely how it will be given, because the drill it is being
# given by is his - taught in a yard, over a winter, to a boy who was bad at it
# for the first two years. There is nothing to guess. He calls the follow-up
# before the movement finishes, twice, and both times the ground is where he
# said it would be. That is why the KIND is `ordered` and why the SWING earns
# bc_pursuit from the verb table: a chase run by a man who knows the quarry's
# footwork is the cleanest and worst thing on a field.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads a relation and the
# side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE, AND THE PRESTIGE IS NOT AN OPINION. The field pays for a
# pursuit that works. Nothing in this template says the afternoon was good.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_foe_is_kin_trigger's MOMENTS. A fostering is the arrangement
# that exists precisely where blood does not - the whole point of sending a boy
# away is that the hall he goes to is not his family's - and a man who fostered
# his own nephew answers yes to both gates for two different reasons.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_old_grudge OR bc_knight_he_has_read_these_terms_before.
# Neither of those men taught the other anything. The knowledge in this moment
# is not a memory of having fought him; it is a memory of having MADE him, and
# it arrives as a prediction rather than as a warning.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE WARD, may not say how long ago the fostering was,
# may not put the yard or the winter on the page as a scene, may not have him
# feel proud, may not have him spare anybody, and may not have the two of them
# see each other. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_he_taught_that_retreat = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_the_boy_he_raised_commands_the_far_side_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The man who was worth fostering a boy with is the man who is good
		# at this, and being good at this is what turns the knowledge into
		# a pursuit rather than a feeling.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = callous
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_he_taught_that_retreat"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_he_taught_that_retreat_toast
			desc = bc_he_taught_that_retreat_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH NINETY-SEVEN. Which way the war travelled.
#
# THE MOD HAS READ THE WAR SCOPE THREE TIMES - how old, what kind, whose
# signature - and never once asked who came to whom. Ninety-six batches, and
# the first question anybody asks about a war has never been in the file.
#
# TWO TEMPLATES, ONE PER POOL, AND NO MAN CAN ANSWER YES TO BOTH GATES. The
# attacker list and the defender list are the two halves of one object; see the
# trigger header for why there is no explicit NOT here and none needed.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The season was his to choose. He is a named attacker, the side is winning.
#
# `measured`/`up`, ONE OF THE THREE SMALLEST CELLS IN THE CENSUS. `measured`
# is the mod's word for reading the afternoon rather than being changed by it,
# and this is the coldest version of that reading available: a man watching a
# thing arrive that he ordered months ago. The verb table hands out nothing for
# it, which is right. Nothing is braced, nothing is bled, nothing is chasing.
# The army is doing what it was pointed at.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the war link and the
# side row and never asks about a rung, so the grant is live.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_war_is_his_trigger'S MOMENT. That gate asks whether he is
# the war leader - whether the quarrel is HIS to settle and there is nobody
# further up to carry the blame. This one asks a different question with a
# different answer set: a vassal called into his liege's invasion is an
# attacker and is nobody's war leader, and a lord defending his own seat is a
# war leader and no kind of attacker. Neither gate is a subset of the other and
# neither argument is the other's. Ownership and direction are separate facts.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_word_they_will_write_it_under EITHER. That man is thinking
# about the account of the afternoon. This one is not thinking about the
# afternoon at all; he is thinking about the winter before it, and the
# afternoon is only the invoice arriving.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE WAR, ITS CAUSE OR ITS CLAIM, may not put a month or
# a year or a count of days on the page, may not have him pleased with himself,
# may not call the arrangement clever, may not have anybody praise the plan to
# him, and may not have any part of the plan fail. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_the_season_was_his_to_choose = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_he_is_the_one_who_crossed_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Choosing a season is a stewardship act done a year early, and the
		# men who do it are the men who can be bothered to.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			stewardship >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = reckless
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_the_season_was_his_to_choose"

		add_prestige = 50

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
			bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = up }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = bc_the_season_was_his_to_choose_toast
			desc = bc_the_season_was_his_to_choose_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# BATCH NINETY-EIGHT. What he is carrying that nobody here can see.
#
# NINETY-SEVEN BATCHES AND THE MOD HAS NEVER OPENED A SECRET. It reads what a
# man took openly, what somebody holds over him, what people think of him, what
# he is related to and married into and was raised by - every one of them a
# fact somebody else could tell you. The engine keeps a list of the facts
# nobody can, and `any_secret` had never been read.
#
# TWO TEMPLATES, ONE PER POOL, AND THE GATES CARRY EACH OTHER'S NEGATION. One
# man's secret is out and one man's is not, and the difference between those
# two afternoons is the batch.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# It goes where he goes. Nobody alive knows it, and the side is losing.
#
# `ordered`/`down` AND NOTHING HANDED OUT, which is the cell this mod keeps for
# a line that holds its shape while going backwards. The shape is real - he is
# not coming apart, he is withdrawing properly - and the reason it is worth a
# template is that the thing occupying the back of his head has no tactical
# content whatsoever. There is nothing for the verb table to say about a man
# who is thinking about something the field cannot see, and handing him an army
# modifier for it would be the mod inventing a consequence.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads two iterations and
# the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THE GATE IS THE ARGUMENT AND IT IS NOT WHAT IT LOOKS LIKE. This is not a man
# afraid of being found out. Nobody can find him out; the list of people who
# could say it aloud is empty and the afternoon is doing its best to keep it
# that way. What is actually in the moment is the arithmetic that arrives with
# that: if this ends the way it is currently going, the only copy goes into the
# ground with him, and he cannot decide, while a day like this one is going on
# around him, whether that is the last piece of luck he owns or the worst
# thing about it.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_his_lord_holds_a_hook_trigger'S MOMENT. That is a man being
# leaned on by somebody standing behind him in the same war. Nobody is leaning
# on this one. A hook is a transaction and this is a condition, and the two
# templates would be about different things even if the same fact underlay both.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_a_room_somewhere_in_tonight EITHER, which is also `ordered`/
# `down` and also about what tonight will be. That man is withdrawing carefully
# because of a person he will have to sit down in front of. This one has
# nobody to sit down in front of, and that is precisely the problem.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT SAY WHAT THE SECRET IS, may not hint at its kind, may not
# name or picture anybody connected to it, may not have him nearly say it, may
# not have anybody ask him anything, and may not resolve it in either
# direction. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_it_goes_where_he_goes = {
	type = commander

	is_valid = {
		bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger = yes
		bc_nobody_alive_knows_it_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 50
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Keeping a thing to yourself for years is a skill, and the men who
		# have it are the men who never had to be told it was one.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			intrigue >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = cynical
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = trusting
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_commander
		bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		bc_mark_chronicle_commander_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_it_goes_where_he_goes"

		add_stress = 10

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
			bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
		}

		bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = down }

		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = bc_it_goes_where_he_goes_toast
			desc = bc_it_goes_where_he_goes_toast_desc
			left_icon = scope:bc_commander
		}
	}
}
